`stepaudio-3-realtime` &#124; 实时语音 &#124; 0.65 元／会话分钟（39.00 元／会话小时） &#124; 按会话时长计费，用户说话和 agent 说话的时间都计入。 最小计价单位为秒，不足 1 秒的部分按 1 秒计算，N 秒加不足 1 秒时按 N+1 秒计费。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; 语音合成 &#124; 95.00 元／百万输入文本计费字符 &#124; 按输入文本的计费字符数计费，1 个英文字母算 1 个字符，1 个日文字符算 2.5 个字符，1 个汉字算 3 个字符，其他字符（包括数字、标点、空格）1 个算 1 个字符。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; 音色复刻 &#124; 10.00 元／个音色 &#124; 用参考音频复刻出一个专属音色。 把音色加入语音库时收费，试听接口只收合成费用。复刻或设计得到的音色用于语音合成时，合成费用按所用模型的语音合成单价另计。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; 音色设计 &#124; 10.00 元／个音色 &#124; 用文本描述生成一个新音色。 把音色加入语音库时收费，试听接口只收合成费用。复刻或设计得到的音色用于语音合成时，合成费用按所用模型的语音合成单价另计。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-gen` &#124; 音频生成 &#124; 95.00 元／百万输入文本计费字符 &#124; 和 `stepaudio-3-tts` 语音合成一样，按输入文本的计费字符数计费，1 个英文字母算 1 个字符，1 个日文字符算 2.5 个字符，1 个汉字算 3 个字符，其他字符（包括数字、标点、空格）1 个算 1 个字符。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-music` &#124; 音乐生成·第一档（无音频输入）：歌曲创作、纯音乐创作 &#124; 16.00 元／输出音频小时 &#124; 按实际生成的音频时长计费，按有无音频输入分两档。最小计价单位为秒，四舍五入为整秒数。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-music` &#124; 音乐生成·第二档（有音频输入）：歌曲改编、清唱配乐 &#124; 32.00 元／输出音频小时 &#124; 按实际生成的音频时长计费，按有无音频输入分两档。最小计价单位为秒，四舍五入为整秒数。 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-asr-max` &#124; 语音识别 &#124; 7.50 元／音频小时 &#124; 按输入音频的时长计费。 &#124;</li><li>### 实时语音与语音对话（按 Token 计费）</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-realtime` &#124; 60.00 &#124; 按量计费 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `step-audio-2` &#124; 10.00 &#124; 2.00 &#124; 70.00 &#124; 按量计费 &#124;</li><li>&#124; `step-audio-r1.5` &#124; 10.00 &#124; 2.00 &#124; 105.00 &#124; 按量计费 &#124;</li><li>### 语音合成</li><li>&#124; | api mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • `POST https://api.stepfun.com/v1/chat/completions`<br>• &gt; Step Plan 场景请使用 `POST https://api.stepfun.com/step_plan/v1/chat/completions`<br>• `step-router-v1` 仅可通过 [Step Plan](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/step-plan/overview.md) 通道（`https://api.stepfun.com/step_plan/v1`）调用，系统会自动在 `step-5-preview` 与 `step-3.7-flash` 之间路由。该模型的字段约束与本文「请求参数」一致，下表列出的字段除外。<br>• &gt; curl https://api.stepfun.com/v1/chat/completions \<br>• &gt; &gt; client = OpenAI(api_key="YOUR_STEP_API_KEY", base_url="https://api.stepfun.com/v1")<br>• &gt; &gt;     baseURL: "https://api.stepfun.com/v1"<br>• &gt; &gt; curl https://api.stepfun.com/v1/chat/completions \ |
| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（94 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li>需要使用的模型名称，例如 `step-5-preview`、`step-3.7-flash`、`step-3.5-flash`、`step-3.5-flash-2603`、`stepaudio-3-chat-preview` 或 `stepaudio-2.5-chat`。`step-router-v1` 仅可通过 [Step Plan](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/step-plan/overview.md) 通道调用，详见本文「Step Plan 通道：Step Router V1 字段差异」。</li><li>结构化的图、视频、音频、文字混合消息</li><li>总为 `image_url`</li><li>* `image_url` `object`</li><li>图片地址或 base64 编码的图片，图片格式：jpg/jpeg、png、webp、静态 gif，仅支持 http 和 https 协议。base64 格式举例：`data:image/jpeg;base64,${base64_string}`。</li><li>图片细节级别：`low` / `high` 可选。`high` 按原图分辨率理解，在大图、OCR、极端长宽比等场景表现更好，token 随图片大小变化；`low` 将图片缩放到固定尺寸、更省 token，适合不要求高清细节、想省 token 或提速的场景。</li><li>* 视频消息 `object`</li><li>总为 `video_url`</li><li>* `video_url` `object`</li><li>视频的 URL 地址，视频格式：video/mp4，仅支持 http 和 https 协议，视频内容要小于 128M，时长建议小于 5 分钟。</li><li>* 音频消息 `object`</li><li>总为 `input_audio`</li><li>* `input_audio` `object`</li><li>音频内容，为音频的 base64 编码。base64 格式举例：`data:audio/mpeg;base64,${base64_string}`，请更换音频格式（当前仅支持 mp3 和 wav）及对应的 base64 编码后字符串。</li><li>* `tool_call_id` `string`\</li><li>Toolcall 支持的函数列表。`step-5-preview` 不支持其中的 `type: "web_search"`，请改用 [网页搜索 API](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/api-reference/search/search.md)。</li><li>* `audio` `object` ***optional***\</li><li>用于控制音频输出的参数，只在支持端到端模型场景的模型下生效（`step-1o-audio`/`step-audio-2`/`step-audio-2-mini`/`step-audio-r1.5`）</li><li>指定生成音频的声音 ID，对于 `step-1o-audio` 可通过[获取声音列表接口](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/api-reference/audio/list-voice.md)查询可用的声音 ID，对于 `step-audio-2` 系列模型，可用 `wenrounansheng`（温柔男声）、`qingchunshaonv`（青春少女）、`livelybreezy-female`（活力少女）、`elegantgentle-female`（高雅女声）四种声音 ID。</li><li>指定生成音频的格式，支持 `pcm` 和 `wav` 两种格式，在非流式场景下（stream=false）支持 wav 输出；在流式场景下（stream=true）时，只支持 pcm 输出（24khz, 单声道, 16bit）。</li><li>指定输出的模态类型，支持 `text`、`audio` 两种模态类型，只在端到端模型场景下必填。如果需要模型输出音频，则需要将 `audio` 添加到该参数中，建议设置为 `["text", "audio"]`。`stepaudio-2.5-chat` 仅支持 `text`，若选择 `audio` 会报错。</li><li>* `reasoning_format` `string` ***optional***\</li><li>用于指导模型输出时使用的 reasoning 字段；默认为 `general`，表示通用推理，使用 `reasoning` 字段返回结果；可选项为</li><li>\[`general`,`deepseek-style`]。当设置为 `deepseek-style` 时，可使用 DeepSeek 兼容的 `reasoning_content` 字段获取到</li><li>reasoning 内容。</li><li>* `reasoning_effort` `string` ***optional***\</li><li>控制模型的推理深度。支持三档推理强度的模型可选值为 `low`、`medium`、`high`；`step-3.5-flash-2603` 兼容 `low`、`high` 两档。值越高，模型会进行更深入的思考，但响应时间可能更长。</li><li>&#124; `messages` 中的图像 / 文档输入 &#124; 不支持，使用会返回 `unsupported_content_type` &#124;</li><li>模型停止继续生成的原因，可选值包括 `stop`（正常结束）、`length`（达到 `max_tokens` 上限）与 `tool_calls`（模型发起工具调用）</li><li>- `reasoning` `string`\</li><li>推理思考过程内容，仅在 step 推理模型下返回。</li><li>- `reasoning_content` `string`\</li><li>推理思考过程内容，与 `reasoning` 字段一同返回且内容一致（DeepSeek 兼容字段名），仅在 step 推理模型下返回。</li><li>- `audio` `object` ***optional***\</li><li>端到端语音模型生成的音频内容，仅当请求参数 `modalities` 包含 `audio` 时返回。</li><li>生成音频的 base64 编码</li><li>* `transcript` `string`\</li><li>生成音频对应的文本转写</li><li>- `tool_calls` `object array` ***optional***\</li><li>toolcall 内容返回</li><li>toolcall 的类型，默认为 `function`</li><li>&amp;#x20;函数执行的参数，一般为 json 结构化对象。</li><li>- `reasoning_tokens` `int`\</li><li>推理思考过程消耗的 token 数量</li><li>"content": "你好！阶跃星辰是一家专注于人工智能技术的公司，致力于开发和提供各种AI解决方案。我们的人工智能技术涵盖了自然语言处理、计算机视觉、机器学习等领域，旨在帮助用户在各个行业和领域中提高效率和创造价值。"</li><li>函数执行的参数，一般为 json 结构化对象。</li><li>&gt; 说明：以下基础示例默认使用 `step-5-preview`。如需控制模型的推理深度，可参考「推理强度」示例中的 `reasoning_effort` 用法。</li><li>&gt; [tab] 推理强度</li><li>&gt;     "reasoning_effort": "medium",</li><li>&gt;         "content": "阶跃星辰（StepFun）是一家专注于**多模态人工智能技术研发与应用**的科技公司，致力于推动大模型（LLM）在文本、图像、逻辑推理等多领域的融合创新。其核心产品 **Step AI**（又称Step模型）具备以下特点：\n\n---\n\n### 🔍 **核心能力**\n1. **多模态融合**  \n   - 不仅能处理文本，还可理解图片、图表等视觉信息，实现跨模态推理（例如根据图片描述内容、解答视觉逻辑题）。\n2. **强大的逻辑与知识能力**  \n   - 在数理逻辑、代码生成、专业知识问答等场景表现突出，支持复杂任务拆解与精准分析。\n3. **多语言支持**  \n   - 覆盖中、英及多语种交流，适应全球化场景。\n4. **安全与合规**  \n   - 严格遵循内容安全规范，拒绝生成暴力、仇恨、虚假信息等有害内容，注重用户隐私保护。\n\n---\n\n### 🌟 **技术亮点**\n- **自主研发大模型架构**：针对多模态任务优化，提升视觉-语言对齐能力。\n- **高效推理与低能耗设计**：在保证性能的同时，</li><li>&gt; &gt;                     "type": "image_url",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                     "image_url": {</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         type: "image_url",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         image_url: {</li><li>&gt; &gt;             "type": "image_url",</li><li>&gt; &gt;             "image_url": {</li><li>&gt; [tab] 语音输入输出</li><li>&gt; &gt;     model="step-audio-2",</li><li>&gt; &gt;     modalities=["text", "audio"],</li><li>&gt; &gt;     audio={</li><li>&gt; &gt; # response transcript</li><li>&gt; &gt; print(completion.choices[0].message.audio.transcript)</li><li>&gt; &gt; # save response audio</li><li>&gt; &gt;     f.write(base64.b64decode(completion.choices[0].message.audio.data))</li><li>&gt; &gt; # 打开 response.wav 即可收听音频回复</li><li>&gt; &gt; const downloadAudioAsBase64 = async (url) =&gt; {</li><li>&gt; &gt;     return "data:audio/wav;base64," + buffer.toString("base64");</li><li>&gt; &gt;         model: "step-audio-2",</li><li>&gt; &gt;         modalities: ["text", "audio"],</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         type: "input_audio",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         input_audio: {</li><li>&gt; &gt;                             data: await downloadAudioAsBase64("https://stepchat-static.tos-cn-shanghai.volces.com/resource_tmp/cat-meow.wav")</li><li>&gt; &gt;         audio: {</li><li>&gt; &gt;     // response transcript</li><li>&gt; &gt;     console.log(completion.choices[0].message.audio.transcript);</li><li>&gt; &gt;     // save response audio</li><li>&gt; &gt;     fs.writeFileSync("response.wav", Buffer.from(completion.choices[0].message.audio.data, "base64"));</li><li>&gt; &gt;     // 打开 response.wav 即可收听音频回复</li><li>&gt; &gt;     "model": "step-audio-2",</li><li>&gt; &gt;     "modalities": ["text", "audio"],</li><li>&gt; &gt;     "audio": {</li><li>&gt; 语音示例返回（text+audio 模态，带 toolcall 字段），由于带有 audio，所以返回内容较大，audio 字段内容被截断显示：</li><li>&gt;         "audio": {</li><li>&gt;           "transcript": "哎呀，现在是下午三点啦。"</li><li>&gt;         "tool_calls": [</li><li>&gt;   "model": "step-audio-2",</li><li>&gt; &gt; image_str = base64.b64encode(r.content).decode("ascii")</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         "url": "data:image/webp;base64,%s" % (image_str),</li><li>&gt; &gt; async function load_image(url) {</li><li>&gt; &gt;                             url: await load_image("https://www.stepfun.com/assets/section-1-CTe4nZiO.webp")</li><li>&gt; &gt; image_base64="data:image/webp;base64,"$(curl -s "https://www.stepfun.com/assets/section-1-CTe4nZiO.webp" &#124; | excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Chat Completions API<br>调用 Chat Completions API，获取模型生成的对话响应数据。<br>## 请求地址<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.com/v1/chat/completions`<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Step Plan 场景请使用 `POST https://api.stepfun.com/step_plan/v1/chat/completions`<br>## 请求参数<br>* `model` `string` ***required***\<br>需要使用的模型名称，例如 `step-5-preview`、`step-3.7-flash`、`step-3.5-flash`、`step-3.5-flash-2603`、`stepaudio-3-chat-preview` 或 `stepaudio-2.5-chat`。`step-router-v1` 仅可通过 [Step Plan](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/step-plan/overview.md) 通道调用，详见本文「Step Plan 通道：Step Router V1 字段差异」。<br>* `messages` `object array` ***required***\<br>迄今为止用户输入或模型生成的不同类别消息列表<br>* 系统消息 `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>系统类别名称，总是为 `system`<br>* `content` `string`\<br>系统消息的文本内容<br>* 用户消息 `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>用户类别名称，总是 `user`<br>* `content` `string or object array`\<br>用户消息内容，类型为 `multipart` 消息列表或者普通文本消息字符串<br>* 普通文本消息 `string`<br>* `multipart` 消息列表 `object array`\<br>结构化的图、视频、音频、文字混合消息<br>* 文本消息 `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>总为 `text`<br>* `text` `string`\<br>消息文本内容<br>* 图片消息 `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>总为 `image_url`<br>* `image_url` `object`<br>* `url` `string`\<br>图片地址或 base64 编码的图片，图片格式：jpg/jpeg、png、webp、静态 gif，仅支持 http 和 https 协议。base64 格式举例：`data:image/jpeg;base64,${base64_string}`。<br>* `detail` `string` ***optional***\<br>图片细节级别：`low` / `high` 可选。`high` 按原图分辨率理解，在大图、OCR、极端长宽比等场景表现更好，token 随图片大小变化；`low` 将图片缩放到固定尺寸、更省 token，适合不要求高清细节、想省 token 或提速的场景。<br>* 视频消息 `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>总为 `video_url`<br>* `video_url` `object`<br>* `url` `string`\<br>视频的 URL 地址，视频格式：video/mp4，仅支持 http 和 https 协议，视频内容要小于 128M，时长建议小于 5 分钟。<br>* 音频消息 `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>总为 `input_audio`<br>* `input_audio` `object`<br>* `data` `string`\<br>音频内容，为音频的 base64 编码。base64 格式举例：`data:audio/mpeg;base64,${base64_string}`，请更换音频格式（当前仅支持 mp3 和 wav）及对应的 base64 编码后字符串。<br>* 工具函数消息 `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>工具类别名称，总是为 `tool`<br>* `content` `string`\<br>函数执行得到的内容<br>* `tool_call_id` `string`\<br>执行函数的 ID，由 assistant 在上一轮对话中返回。<br>* 聊天助手消息 `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>聊天助手类别名称，总是为 `assistant`<br>* `content` `string &#124; null`\<br>聊天助手消息的文本内容<br>* `tools` `object array` ***optional***\<br>Toolcall 支持的函数列表。`step-5-preview` 不支持其中的 `type: "web_search"`，请改用 [网页搜索 API](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/api-reference/search/search.md)。<br>* `type` `string`\<br>工具类型，总是为 `function`<br>* `function` `object`\<br>函数内容的描述<br>* `name` `string`\<br>函数名称，要求纯英文、数字和 \_- 符号，建议不要超过 64 个字符长度。<br>* `description` `string`\<br>函数描述，支持中英文，它用于告诉模型函数实现何种功能和目的，方便模型来判断和选择。<br>* `parameters` `object`\<br>函数的参数<br>* `type` `object`\<br>参数描述，一般为 object<br>* `properties` `object`\<br>函数参数内容，以 key 为参数的名称，然后通过 `type` 和 `description` 描述参数的类型和介绍。<br>* `type` `string&#124;number&#124;integer&#124;object&#124;array&#124;boolean`\<br>参数类型，可以参考 [json-schema](https://json-schema.org/understanding-json-schema/reference/type) 介绍。<br>* `description` `string`\<br>函数参数描述，支持中英文，它用于告诉模型函数参数的含义。<br>* `audio` `object` ***optional***\<br>用于控制音频输出的参数，只在支持端到端模型场景的模型下生效（`step-1o-audio`/`step-audio-2`/`step-audio-2-mini`/`step-audio-r1.5`）<br>* `voice` `string` ***required***\<br>指定生成音频的声音 ID，对于 `step-1o-audio` 可通过[获取声音列表接口](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/api-reference/audio/list-voice.md)查询可用的声音 ID，对于 `step-audio-2` 系列模型，可用 `wenrounansheng`（温柔男声）、`qingchunshaonv`（青春少女）、`livelybreezy-female`（活力少女）、`elegantgentle-female`（高雅女声）四种声音 ID。<br>* `format` `string` ***required***\<br>指定生成音频的格式，支持 `pcm` 和 `wav` 两种格式，在非流式场景下（stream=false）支持 wav 输出；在流式场景下（stream=true）时，只支持 pcm 输出（24khz, 单声道, 16bit）。<br>* `modalities` `string array` ***optional***\<br>指定输出的模态类型，支持 `text`、`audio` 两种模态类型，只在端到端模型场景下必填。如果需要模型输出音频，则需要将 `audio` 添加到该参数中，建议设置为 `["text", "audio"]`。`stepaudio-2.5-chat` 仅支持 `text`，若选择 `audio` 会报错。<br>* `max_tokens` `int` ***optional***\<br>聊天需要生成的 token 最大数量，默认值为 INF（不作限制，由模型自动决定）。输入 token 和生成 token 的总数量受限于指定模型的最大上下文长度。<br>* `temperature` `float` ***optional***\<br>采样温度，介于0.0和2.0之间的数字。较高值（如0.8）会使生成更随机，较低值（如0.2）会使其生成结果更集中且确定。<br>* `top_p` `float` ***optional***\<br>核心采样，该值会使模型生成具有 top\_p 概率质量的 token 并输出到结果。<br>* `n` `int` ***optional***\ |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（36 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li>### Pricing for Multimodal Reasoning Models</li><li>For `step-5-preview`, the cache-miss input price includes writing new content to the cache. Output tokens include both the model's reasoning process and final answer. See [Prompt caching](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/prompt-cache.md) for details.</li><li>### Pricing for Reasoning Models</li><li>### Pricing for Vision Models</li><li>&#124; `step-1o-turbo-vision` &#124; 1M tokens &#124; \$0.36 &#124; \$0.07 &#124; \$1.15 &#124;</li><li>Image input is counted and billed as tokens, just like text input. By default, each image uses 169 input tokens; with `detail` enabled, the token count is calculated from the image size.</li><li>### Pricing for Speech Models</li><li>**\[Pricing Notice]: The new prices for StepAudio 3 series models and related services listed below take effect at 00:00:00 on November 9, 2026 (UTC+8). Until then, models and services that are already billed will retain their existing rates, while models covered by the limited-time free offer will remain free through 23:59:59 on November 8, 2026 (UTC+8). In addition, due to rising compute costs, we have adjusted the pricing of certain voice models to continue ensuring service stability and infe</li><li>### StepAudio 3 Series</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-realtime` &#124; Realtime speech &#124; \$0.125 / session minute (\$7.50 / session hour) &#124; Billed by session duration. Both user speaking time and agent speaking time are counted. The minimum billing unit is one second. Any partial second is billed as a full second; N seconds plus a partial second is billed as N+1 seconds. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; Text-to-speech &#124; \$14.00 / 1M billable input text characters &#124; Billed by billable characters of the input text. One English letter counts as 1 character, one Japanese character counts as 2.5 characters, one Chinese character counts as 3 characters, and other characters (including digits, punctuation, and spaces) count as 1 character each. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; Voice cloning &#124; \$1.50 / voice &#124; Creates a custom voice from reference audio. Charged when the voice is added to the voice library; trial calls are charged only for synthesis. When a cloned or designed voice is used for speech synthesis, synthesis is charged separately at the text-to-speech unit price of the model used. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; Voice design &#124; \$1.50 / voice &#124; Creates a new voice from a text description. Charged when the voice is added to the voice library; trial calls are charged only for synthesis. When a cloned or designed voice is used for speech synthesis, synthesis is charged separately at the text-to-speech unit price of the model used. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-gen` &#124; Audio generation &#124; \$14.00 / 1M billable input text characters &#124; Same as `stepaudio-3-tts`, billed by billable characters of the input text. One English letter counts as 1 character, one Japanese character counts as 2.5 characters, one Chinese character counts as 3 characters, and other characters (including digits, punctuation, and spaces) count as 1 character each. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-music` &#124; Music generation · Tier 1 (no audio input): Song Creation, Instrumental &#124; \$2.40 / output audio hour &#124; Billed by the duration of the generated audio, in two tiers depending on whether audio input is provided. The minimum billing unit is one second, rounded to the nearest whole second. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-music` &#124; Music generation · Tier 2 (with audio input): Song Adaptation, Vocal Accompaniment &#124; \$4.80 / output audio hour &#124; Billed by the duration of the generated audio, in two tiers depending on whether audio input is provided. The minimum billing unit is one second, rounded to the nearest whole second. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-3-asr-max` &#124; Speech recognition &#124; \$0.60 / audio hour &#124; Billed by the duration of the input audio. &#124;</li><li>### Realtime Voice &amp; Voice Chat (Billed by Token)</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-realtime` &#124; \$1.50 &#124; \$0.30 &#124; \$10.00 &#124; Usage-based &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-chat` &#124; \$1.50 &#124; \$0.30 &#124; \$3.50 &#124; Usage-based &#124;</li><li>&#124; `step-1o-audio` &#124; \$3.57 &#124; \$0.71 &#124; \$8.57 &#124; Usage-based &#124;</li><li>&#124; `step-audio-2` &#124; \$1.43 &#124; \$0.29 &#124; \$10.00 &#124; Usage-based &#124;</li><li>&#124; `step-audio-r1.5` &#124; \$1.43 &#124; \$0.29 &#124; \$15.00 &#124; Usage-based &#124;</li><li>### Text-to-Speech</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-tts` &#124; \$85.00 / 1M characters &#124;</li><li>&gt; `stepaudio-2.5-tts`, `step-tts-2`, `step-tts-mini`: one Chinese character counts as one character, two English letters count as one character, and two punctuation marks count as one character.</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-tts`, `step-tts-2`, `step-tts-mini` &#124; Voice cloning &#124; \$1.50 / voice; charged when the voice is added to the voice library, and trial calls are charged only for synthesis &#124;</li><li>&gt; Voice cloning: creates a custom voice from reference audio. Charged when the voice is added to the voice library; trial calls are charged only for synthesis.</li><li>&gt; When a cloned or designed voice is used for speech synthesis, synthesis is charged separately at the text-to-speech unit price of the model used.</li><li>### Speech Recognition</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-asr` &#124; \$0.05 / hour &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream` &#124; \$0.40 / hour &#124;</li><li>&#124; `stepaudio-2-asr-pro` &#124; \$0.29 / hour &#124;</li><li>### Pricing for Image Generation and Editing</li><li>&gt; `step-2x-large` and `step-image-edit-2` were retired on October 10, 2026. The text-to-image, image-to-image, and image editing APIs have stopped serving requests. See the [image model retirement notice](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/image-offline-notice.md).</li><li>&#124; Image search &#124; \$0.02 / call &#124;</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Pricing and Rate Limits<br>## Pricing Details<br>### Pricing for Multimodal Reasoning Models<br>&#124; Model &#124; Billing unit &#124; Input (cache miss) &#124; Input (cache hit) &#124; Output &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; `step-5-preview` &#124; 1M tokens &#124; \$1.00 &#124; \$0.10 &#124; \$2.70 &#124;<br>&#124; `step-3.7-flash` &#124; 1M tokens &#124; \$0.20 &#124; \$0.04 &#124; \$1.15 &#124;<br>For `step-5-preview`, the cache-miss input price includes writing new content to the cache. Output tokens include both the model's reasoning process and final answer. See [Prompt caching](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/prompt-cache.md) for details.<br>### Pricing for Reasoning Models<br>&#124; Model &#124; Billing unit &#124; Input (cache miss) &#124; Input (cache hit) &#124; Output &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; `step-3.5-flash` &#124; 1M tokens &#124; \$0.10 &#124; \$0.02 &#124; \$0.30 &#124;<br>&#124; `step-3.5-flash-2603` &#124; 1M tokens &#124; \$0.10 &#124; \$0.02 &#124; \$0.30 &#124;<br>### Pricing for Vision Models<br>&#124; Model &#124; Billing unit &#124; Input (cache miss) &#124; Input (cache hit) &#124; Output &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; `step-1o-turbo-vision` &#124; 1M tokens &#124; \$0.36 &#124; \$0.07 &#124; \$1.15 &#124;<br>Image input is counted and billed as tokens, just like text input. By default, each image uses 169 input tokens; with `detail` enabled, the token count is calculated from the image size.<br>### Pricing for Speech Models<br>**\[Pricing Notice]: The new prices for StepAudio 3 series models and related services listed below take effect at 00:00:00 on November 9, 2026 (UTC+8). Until then, models and services that are already billed will retain their existing rates, while models covered by the limited-time free offer will remain free through 23:59:59 on November 8, 2026 (UTC+8). In addition, due to rising compute costs, we have adjusted the pricing of certain voice models to continue ensuring service stability and inference quality. Those adjusted prices also take effect at 00:00:00 on November 9, 2026 (UTC+8). Charges are billed in US dollars (USD).**<br>### StepAudio 3 Series<br>&#124; Model ID &#124; Capability &#124; Unit price &#124; Notes &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-realtime` &#124; Realtime speech &#124; \$0.125 / session minute (\$7.50 / session hour) &#124; Billed by session duration. Both user speaking time and agent speaking time are counted. The minimum billing unit is one second. Any partial second is billed as a full second; N seconds plus a partial second is billed as N+1 seconds. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; Text-to-speech &#124; \$14.00 / 1M billable input text characters &#124; Billed by billable characters of the input text. One English letter counts as 1 character, one Japanese character counts as 2.5 characters, one Chinese character counts as 3 characters, and other characters (including digits, punctuation, and spaces) count as 1 character each. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; Voice cloning &#124; \$1.50 / voice &#124; Creates a custom voice from reference audio. Charged when the voice is added to the voice library; trial calls are charged only for synthesis. When a cloned or designed voice is used for speech synthesis, synthesis is charged separately at the text-to-speech unit price of the model used. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-tts` &#124; Voice design &#124; \$1.50 / voice &#124; Creates a new voice from a text description. Charged when the voice is added to the voice library; trial calls are charged only for synthesis. When a cloned or designed voice is used for speech synthesis, synthesis is charged separately at the text-to-speech unit price of the model used. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-gen` &#124; Audio generation &#124; \$14.00 / 1M billable input text characters &#124; Same as `stepaudio-3-tts`, billed by billable characters of the input text. One English letter counts as 1 character, one Japanese character counts as 2.5 characters, one Chinese character counts as 3 characters, and other characters (including digits, punctuation, and spaces) count as 1 character each. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-music` &#124; Music generation · Tier 1 (no audio input): Song Creation, Instrumental &#124; \$2.40 / output audio hour &#124; Billed by the duration of the generated audio, in two tiers depending on whether audio input is provided. The minimum billing unit is one second, rounded to the nearest whole second. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-music` &#124; Music generation · Tier 2 (with audio input): Song Adaptation, Vocal Accompaniment &#124; \$4.80 / output audio hour &#124; Billed by the duration of the generated audio, in two tiers depending on whether audio input is provided. The minimum billing unit is one second, rounded to the nearest whole second. &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-3-asr-max` &#124; Speech recognition &#124; \$0.60 / audio hour &#124; Billed by the duration of the input audio. &#124;<br>### Other Models<br>### Realtime Voice &amp; Voice Chat (Billed by Token)<br>Unit: USD / 1M tokens.<br>&#124; Model ID &#124; Input Price (Cache Miss) &#124; Input Price (Cache Hit) &#124; Output Price &#124; Status &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-realtime` &#124; \$1.50 &#124; \$0.30 &#124; \$10.00 &#124; Usage-based &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-chat` &#124; \$1.50 &#124; \$0.30 &#124; \$3.50 &#124; Usage-based &#124;<br>&#124; `step-1o-audio` &#124; \$3.57 &#124; \$0.71 &#124; \$8.57 &#124; Usage-based &#124;<br>&#124; `step-audio-2` &#124; \$1.43 &#124; \$0.29 &#124; \$10.00 &#124; Usage-based &#124;<br>&#124; `step-audio-r1.5` &#124; \$1.43 &#124; \$0.29 &#124; \$15.00 &#124; Usage-based &#124;<br>### Text-to-Speech<br>&#124; Model ID &#124; Unit price &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-tts` &#124; \$85.00 / 1M characters &#124;<br>&#124; `step-tts-2` &#124; \$40.00 / 1M characters &#124;<br>&#124; `step-tts-mini` &#124; \$13.00 / 1M characters &#124;<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; `stepaudio-2.5-tts`, `step-tts-2`, `step-tts-mini`: one Chinese character counts as one character, two English letters count as one character, and two punctuation marks count as one character.<br>### Voice Cloning &amp; Voice Design<br>&#124; Model ID &#124; Capability &#124; Unit price &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-tts`, `step-tts-2`, `step-tts-mini` &#124; Voice cloning &#124; \$1.50 / voice; charged when the voice is added to the voice library, and trial calls are charged only for synthesis &#124;<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Voice cloning: creates a custom voice from reference audio. Charged when the voice is added to the voice library; trial calls are charged only for synthesis.<br>&gt;<br>&gt; When a cloned or designed voice is used for speech synthesis, synthesis is charged separately at the text-to-speech unit price of the model used.<br>### Speech Recognition<br>&#124; Model ID &#124; Unit price &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-asr` &#124; \$0.05 / hour &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream` &#124; \$0.40 / hour &#124;<br>&#124; `stepaudio-2-asr-pro` &#124; \$0.29 / hour &#124;<br>&#124; `step-asr` &#124; \$0.13 / hour &#124;<br>&#124; `step-asr-1.1` &#124; \$0.31 / hour &#124;<br>&#124; `step-asr-1.1-stream` &#124; \$0.81 / hour &#124;<br>### Pricing for Image Generation and Editing<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; `step-2x-large` and `step-image-edit-2` were retired on October 10, 2026. The text-to-image, image-to-image, and image editing APIs have stopped serving requests. See the [image model retirement notice](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/image-offline-notice.md).<br>### Pricing for Value-Added Capabilities<br>&#124; Capability &#124; Billing unit &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; Internet search &#124; \$0.006 / call &#124;<br>&#124; Image search &#124; \$0.02 / call &#124;<br>&#124; File storage &#124; \$0.08 / GB / day &#124;<br>Value-added capabilities are billed by actual usage.<br>## Tiered Rate Limits<br>Pay-as-you-go calls to the standard Open Platform API are rate limited by tier according to the cumulative top-up amount on your account. Only cash top-ups count toward that amount; vouchers do not. [Step Plan](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md) usage is governed by the monthly Credit allowance of your subscribed tier rather than by cumulative top-up. When resources reach capacity, we may temporarily adjust the limits for each tier.<br>### Individual Accounts<br>Individual accounts must complete identity verification first. Tiers are assigned by cumulative top-up amount:<br>&#124; User tier &#124; Cumulative top-up amount &#124; Concurrency &#124; RPM &#124; TPM &#124;<br>&#124; :-: &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; V0 &#124; Under \$15 &#124; 5 &#124; 100 &#124; 500,000 &#124;<br>&#124; V1 &#124; \$15 to \$69 &#124; 20 &#124; 400 &#124; 2,000,000 &#124;<br>&#124; V2 &#124; \$70 to \$299 &#124; 30 &#124; 600 &#124; 3,000,000 &#124;<br>&#124; V3 &#124; \$300 to \$1,499 &#124; 40 &#124; 800 &#124; 4,000,000 &#124;<br>&#124; V4 &#124; \$1,500 and above &#124; 130 &#124; 2,600 &#124; 13,000,000 &#124;<br>### Enterprise Accounts<br>For the rate limit tiers available to verified enterprise accounts, contact your sales representative. Prepaid funds added to an Open Platform account count toward the cumulative top-up amount and are eligible for invoicing through the Open Platform. Postpaid billing is not tied to the cumulative top-up amount and is not invoiced through the Open Platform.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（31 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li># Speech Recognition (Streaming Output)</li><li>HTTP + SSE based speech recognition: submit the audio once, and the server continuously pushes incremental and final transcription results over SSE. Suitable for server-side calls, file transcription, and near-real-time processing. Recommended model: `stepaudio-3-asr-max`.</li><li>&gt; For WebSocket-based real-time bidirectional streaming recognition, see [Streaming Speech Recognition (Bidirectional Streaming)](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/asr-stream.md).</li><li>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/asr/sse`</li><li>&gt; For Step Plan, use `POST https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/audio/asr/sse`.</li><li>* `audio` `object` ***required***\</li><li>Audio data and recognition configuration.</li><li>Base64-encoded audio data.</li><li>Recognition and audio format configuration.</li><li>* `transcription` `object`\</li><li>Model name. Supports `stepaudio-3-asr-max` and `stepaudio-2.5-asr`.</li><li>Audio format.</li><li>Audio container format. Supports `ogg`, `mp3`, `wav`, `pcm`, `m4a`.</li><li>"audio": {</li><li>"data": "audioData",</li><li>"transcription": {</li><li>"model": "stepaudio-3-asr-max",</li><li>&gt; Compatibility note: The SSE endpoint no longer supports the `full_rerun_on_commit` (second-pass correction) parameter. If legacy clients still send it, the server ignores it without affecting the recognition result. For second-pass correction, use the WebSocket endpoint (see [Streaming Speech Recognition (Bidirectional Streaming)](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/asr-stream.md)).</li><li>* Audio data must be Base64-encoded.</li><li>* Supported audio formats: `ogg`, `mp3`, `wav`, `pcm`, `m4a`.</li><li>* When the audio format is `pcm`, `rate`, `bits`, and `channel` are required; for `ogg`, `mp3`, `wav`, and `m4a` they are optional.</li><li>### Delta event (transcript.text.delta)</li><li>Incremental transcription text.</li><li>"type": "transcript.text.delta",</li><li>Event type. Fixed as `transcript.text.delta`.</li><li>### Done event (transcript.text.done)</li><li>The complete transcription text has been generated.</li><li>"type": "transcript.text.done",</li><li>"audio_tokens": 1000</li><li>Event type. Fixed as `transcript.text.done`.</li><li>The complete transcription text.</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Speech Recognition (Streaming Output)<br>HTTP + SSE based speech recognition: submit the audio once, and the server continuously pushes incremental and final transcription results over SSE. Suitable for server-side calls, file transcription, and near-real-time processing. Recommended model: `stepaudio-3-asr-max`.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For WebSocket-based real-time bidirectional streaming recognition, see [Streaming Speech Recognition (Bidirectional Streaming)](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/asr-stream.md).<br>## Endpoint<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/asr/sse`<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For Step Plan, use `POST https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/audio/asr/sse`.<br>## Request Headers<br>* `Content-Type` `string` ***required***\<br>Must be `application/json`.<br>* `Accept` `string` ***required***\<br>Must be `text/event-stream`.<br>* `Authorization` `string` ***required***\<br>Authentication token in the format `Bearer $STEPFUN_API_KEY`.<br>## Request Parameters<br>* `audio` `object` ***required***\<br>Audio data and recognition configuration.<br>* `data` `string` ***required***\<br>Base64-encoded audio data.<br>* `input` `object` ***required***\<br>Recognition and audio format configuration.<br>* `transcription` `object`\<br>Recognition configuration.<br>* `language` `string`\<br>Target language; defaults to English when omitted. Supported values: `zh`, `en`, `ja`, `ko`, `fr`, `es`. Japanese, Korean, French, and Spanish are in preview.<br>* `model` `string`\<br>Model name. Supports `stepaudio-3-asr-max` and `stepaudio-2.5-asr`.<br>* `enable_itn` `bool` ***optional***\<br>Whether to enable ITN (inverse text normalization). Default `true`.<br>* `format` `object`\<br>Audio format.<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Audio container format. Supports `ogg`, `mp3`, `wav`, `pcm`, `m4a`.<br>* `codec` `string`\<br>Codec; when `type=pcm`, typically `pcm_s16le`.<br>* `rate` `int`\<br>Sample rate; required for `pcm`, optional for other formats.<br>* `bits` `int`\<br>Bit depth; required for `pcm`, optional for other formats.<br>* `channel` `int`\<br>Channel count; required for `pcm`, optional for other formats.<br>### Request Example<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"audio": {<br>"data": "audioData",<br>"input": {<br>"transcription": {<br>"language": "zh",<br>"model": "stepaudio-3-asr-max",<br>"enable_itn": true<br>},<br>"format": {<br>"type": "pcm",<br>"codec": "pcm_s16le",<br>"rate": 16000,<br>"bits": 16,<br>"channel": 1<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Compatibility note: The SSE endpoint no longer supports the `full_rerun_on_commit` (second-pass correction) parameter. If legacy clients still send it, the server ignores it without affecting the recognition result. For second-pass correction, use the WebSocket endpoint (see [Streaming Speech Recognition (Bidirectional Streaming)](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/asr-stream.md)).<br>Additional notes:<br>* Audio data must be Base64-encoded.<br>* Supported audio formats: `ogg`, `mp3`, `wav`, `pcm`, `m4a`.<br>* When the audio format is `pcm`, `rate`, `bits`, and `channel` are required; for `ogg`, `mp3`, `wav`, and `m4a` they are optional.<br>## Response<br>SSE streaming response with the following event types.<br>### Delta event (transcript.text.delta)<br>Incremental transcription text.<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"type": "transcript.text.delta",<br>"meta": {<br>"session_id": "sse_1642694400123456789",<br>"timestamp": 1642694400123<br>},<br>"delta": "recognized "<br>}<br>```<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Event type. Fixed as `transcript.text.delta`.<br>* `meta.session_id` `string`\<br>Session ID.<br>* `meta.timestamp` `int64`\<br>Server-side event Unix timestamp, in milliseconds.<br>* `delta` `string`\<br>Incremental transcription text.<br>### Done event (transcript.text.done)<br>The complete transcription text has been generated. |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（55 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li># Streaming Speech Recognition (Bidirectional Streaming)</li><li>WebSocket-based bidirectional streaming speech recognition: send audio chunks in real time and continuously receive incremental and final transcription results. Supports server-side VAD (voice activity detection), suitable for real-time conversations, voice assistants, live captioning, and similar scenarios. Recommended model: `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream`.</li><li>&gt; To submit audio once over HTTP + SSE and receive streaming recognition results, see [Speech Recognition (Streaming Output)](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/asr-sse.md).</li><li>Connect via WebSocket: `wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/asr/stream`</li><li>Update the session configuration, including audio format and recognition parameters.</li><li>"audio": {</li><li>"transcription": {</li><li>"model": "stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream",</li><li>"prompt": "Please transcribe the speech you hear.",</li><li>* `session.audio.input.format` `object`\</li><li>Audio format.</li><li>Audio container format. Supports `pcm`, `ogg`.</li><li>Audio codec; use `pcm_s16le` when `type=pcm`.</li><li>* `session.audio.input.transcription` `object`\</li><li>Transcription model name. Supports `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream`.</li><li>* `session.audio.input.turn_detection` `object`\</li><li>* Currently supports `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream`.</li><li>* If `turn_detection.type=server_vad` is not set, the server does not perform VAD automatically; in that case the client must actively send `input_audio_buffer.commit`.</li><li>### Append audio (input\_audio\_buffer.append)</li><li>Send audio data for real-time recognition.</li><li>"type": "input_audio_buffer.append",</li><li>"audio": "base64_encoded_audio_data"</li><li>* `audio` `string` ***required***\</li><li>Base64-encoded audio data (WAV format).</li><li>### Commit buffer (input\_audio\_buffer.commit)</li><li>Send this message only when `server_vad` is disabled, to ask the server to commit the audio buffer and trigger transcription.</li><li>"type": "input_audio_buffer.commit"</li><li>### Speech started (input\_audio\_buffer.speech\_started)</li><li>Returned only when `server_vad` is enabled; indicates the server detected the start of speech.</li><li>"type": "input_audio_buffer.speech_started",</li><li>"audio_start_ms": 15240,</li><li>### Speech stopped (input\_audio\_buffer.speech\_stopped)</li><li>Returned only when `server_vad` is enabled; indicates the server detected the end of speech.</li><li>"type": "input_audio_buffer.speech_stopped",</li><li>"audio_start_ms": 13480,</li><li>### Buffer committed (input\_audio\_buffer.committed)</li><li>Confirms the audio buffer was committed.</li><li>"type": "input_audio_buffer.committed",</li><li>A new conversation item (transcription result) has been created.</li><li>"object": "realtime.item",</li><li>"type": "input_audio"</li><li>### Transcription delta (conversation.item.input\_audio\_transcription.delta)</li><li>Returns incremental transcription results (streaming output).</li><li>"type": "conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.delta",</li><li>### Transcription completed (conversation.item.input\_audio\_transcription.completed)</li><li>Returns the complete transcription result.</li><li>"type": "conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed",</li><li>"transcript": "你好，世界",</li><li>* `transcript` `string`\</li><li>The complete transcription text.</li><li>Detected language of the transcription.</li><li>The reason the transcription was finalized, e.g. `commit`.</li><li>"param": "audio",</li><li>* No recognition result: verify the audio format, codec, and quality.</li><li>* High latency: reduce the audio chunk size.</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Streaming Speech Recognition (Bidirectional Streaming)<br>WebSocket-based bidirectional streaming speech recognition: send audio chunks in real time and continuously receive incremental and final transcription results. Supports server-side VAD (voice activity detection), suitable for real-time conversations, voice assistants, live captioning, and similar scenarios. Recommended model: `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream`.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; To submit audio once over HTTP + SSE and receive streaming recognition results, see [Speech Recognition (Streaming Output)](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/asr-sse.md).<br>## Endpoint<br>Connect via WebSocket: `wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/asr/stream`<br>## Authentication<br>* `Authorization` `string` ***required***\<br>Authentication token in the format `Bearer $STEPFUN_API_KEY`.<br>## Client Messages<br>### Session update (session.update)<br>Update the session configuration, including audio format and recognition parameters.<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"event_id": "event_123",<br>"type": "session.update",<br>"session": {<br>"audio": {<br>"input": {<br>"format": {<br>"type": "pcm",<br>"codec": "pcm_s16le",<br>"rate": 16000,<br>"bits": 16,<br>"channel": 1<br>},<br>"transcription": {<br>"model": "stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream",<br>"language": "zh",<br>"prompt": "Please transcribe the speech you hear.",<br>"full_rerun_on_commit": true,<br>"enable_itn": true<br>},<br>"turn_detection": {<br>"type": "server_vad",<br>"silence_duration_ms": 800,<br>"threshold": 0.5<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>* `event_id` `string` ***required***\<br>Event ID of the current message.<br>* `type` `string` ***required***\<br>Message type. Fixed as `session.update`.<br>* `session.audio.input.format` `object`\<br>Audio format.<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Audio container format. Supports `pcm`, `ogg`.<br>* `codec` `string`\<br>Audio codec; use `pcm_s16le` when `type=pcm`.<br>* `rate` `integer`\<br>Sample rate.<br>* `bits` `integer`\<br>Bit depth.<br>* `channel` `integer`\<br>Channel count.<br>* `session.audio.input.transcription` `object`\<br>Recognition configuration.<br>* `model` `string`\<br>Transcription model name. Supports `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream`.<br>* `language` `string`\<br>Recognition language; defaults to English when omitted. Supported values: `zh`, `en`, `ja`, `ko`, `fr`, `es`. Japanese, Korean, French, and Spanish are in preview.<br>* `prompt` `string`\<br>Prompt used to provide additional context or domain-specific terminology.<br>* `full_rerun_on_commit` `boolean`\<br>Whether to run a second-pass correction after commit. Default `false`.<br>* `enable_itn` `boolean`\<br>Whether to enable ITN (inverse text normalization).<br>* `session.audio.input.turn_detection` `object`\<br>Voice activity detection (VAD) configuration.<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Detection type. Supports `server_vad`.<br>* `silence_duration_ms` `integer`\<br>Silence threshold; a sentence is considered finished after this duration of silence.<br>* `threshold` `number`\<br>VAD detection threshold; the higher the value, the stricter the detection.<br>Additional notes:<br>* Currently supports `stepaudio-2.5-asr-stream`.<br>* If `turn_detection.type=server_vad` is not set, the server does not perform VAD automatically; in that case the client must actively send `input_audio_buffer.commit`.<br>### Append audio (input\_audio\_buffer.append)<br>Send audio data for real-time recognition.<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"event_id": "event_124",<br>"type": "input_audio_buffer.append",<br>"audio": "base64_encoded_audio_data"<br>}<br>```<br>* `event_id` `string` ***required***\<br>Unique event identifier.<br>* `audio` `string` ***required***\<br>Base64-encoded audio data (WAV format).<br>### Commit buffer (input\_audio\_buffer.commit) |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（14 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li># Audio Generation</li><li>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/generate`</li><li>Model name. Currently supports `stepaudio-3-gen-preview`.</li><li>Task type. Currently only `text_to_audio` is supported: design a voice with a natural-language description and generate voices, sound effects, background music, and other sound elements. When using this task, at least one of `scripts` or `instruction` is required; `roles` is optional.</li><li>Returned audio format. Supports `wav`, `mp3`, `flac`, `opus`, and `pcm`.</li><li>Speech speed. Range: 0.5 to 2.</li><li>Return mode. Default `audio`: `audio` returns the generated audio directly; `sse` continuously returns Base64-encoded audio chunks via Server-Sent Events.</li><li>Whether to return the audio URL.</li><li>&gt; The reference-voice task (`reference_to_audio`), which synthesizes audio using an official or cloned voice, is not yet available; it will be added to this page once supported.</li><li>### Audio generation</li><li>curl --location 'https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/generate' \</li><li>"model": "stepaudio-3-gen-preview",</li><li>"task": "text_to_audio",</li><li>* `stepaudio-3-gen-preview` does not support the `voice`, `voice_label`, or `timestamp` parameters.</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Audio Generation<br>Generate a wide range of sound elements—voices, sound effects, ambient sound, and background music—in a unified way from text descriptions.<br>## Endpoint<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/generate`<br>## Request Headers<br>* `Content-Type` `string` ***required***\<br>Fixed as `application/json`.<br>* `Authorization` `string` ***required***\<br>Authentication token in the format `Bearer $STEP_API_KEY`.<br>## Request Parameters<br>* `model` `string` ***required***\<br>Model name. Currently supports `stepaudio-3-gen-preview`.<br>* `task` `string` ***required***\<br>Task type. Currently only `text_to_audio` is supported: design a voice with a natural-language description and generate voices, sound effects, background music, and other sound elements. When using this task, at least one of `scripts` or `instruction` is required; `roles` is optional.<br>* `roles` `array` ***optional***\<br>The roles and voice descriptions used for generation. All `name` and `description` values together can be at most 500 characters.<br>* `name` `string` ***optional***\<br>A custom role or voice name, e.g. "Lead".<br>* `description` `string` ***optional***\<br>A custom role or voice description, e.g. "A man in his twenties, a slightly husky voice, with full breath when singing." `name` and `description` must both be provided or both left empty.<br>* `scripts` `array` ***optional***\<br>The lines, sound-effect, or background-music descriptions to generate, at most 1,000 characters in total. A spoken line specifies its speaker via `speaker` and can use `()` to describe tone, style, and emotion; wrap sound-effect and background-music descriptions in `[]`, in which case `speaker` can be omitted.<br>* `speaker` `string` ***optional***\<br>The `roles.name` the line belongs to. Can be omitted for pure sound-effect or BGM descriptions.<br>* `text` `string` ***required***\<br>A spoken line, sound effect, or BGM description.<br>* `instruction` `string` ***optional***\<br>Global natural-language guidance used to set the environment, BGM, and emotional tone. Up to 500 characters.<br>* `response_format` `string` ***optional***\<br>Returned audio format. Supports `wav`, `mp3`, `flac`, `opus`, and `pcm`.<br>* `speed` `number` ***optional***\<br>Speech speed. Range: 0.5 to 2.<br>* `volume` `number` ***optional***\<br>Volume. Range: 0.1 to 2.0.<br>* `sample_rate` `integer` ***optional***\<br>Sampling rate. Supports `8000`, `16000`, `22050`, `24000`, and `48000`.<br>* `pronunciation_map` `object` ***optional***\<br>Pronunciation replacement rules.<br>* `text_normalization` `string` ***optional***\<br>Text normalization strategy. Supports `standard` and `enhanced`.<br>* `stream_format` `string` ***optional***\<br>Return mode. Default `audio`: `audio` returns the generated audio directly; `sse` continuously returns Base64-encoded audio chunks via Server-Sent Events.<br>* `return_url` `boolean` ***optional***\<br>Whether to return the audio URL.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; The reference-voice task (`reference_to_audio`), which synthesizes audio using an official or cloned voice, is not yet available; it will be added to this page once supported.<br>## Request Example<br>### Audio generation<br>```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>curl --location 'https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/generate' \<br>--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \<br>--header 'Authorization: Bearer '"$STEP_API_KEY" \<br>--data '{<br>"model": "stepaudio-3-gen-preview",<br>"task": "text_to_audio",<br>"roles": [<br>{ "name": "Ground crew", "description": "A man around thirty, crisp and efficient voice, fast pace, focused on apron work, raising his volume slightly to be heard over the engine noise" },<br>{ "name": "Captain", "description": "A middle-aged man, voice coming over the radio with the electronic compression and background static typical of aviation comms, calm and professional" },<br>{ "name": "Tower", "description": "A woman, voice also over the radio, clear and articulate with slight distortion, calmly issuing instructions" }<br>],<br>"scripts": [<br>{ "text": "[The steady high-pitched whine of an airliner APU, mixed with the low rumble of a tug engine]" },<br>{ "speaker": "Ground crew", "text": "(into the headset, raising volume over the noise) Left engine intake check complete, chocks removed, ready for pushback." },<br>{ "speaker": "Captain", "text": "(radio voice, compressed and distorted) Roger, requesting pushback approval, watching for ground signals." },<br>{ "text": "[The radio clicks on with a burst of static, carrying a voice over the current]" },<br>{ "speaker": "Tower", "text": "(radio voice, calm) Pushback approved, runway two-three right, surface wind zero-nine-zero at three knots, have a good flight." },<br>{ "speaker": "Ground crew", "text": "(waving the marshalling wands, responding loudly) Understood, tug starting pushback, mind the left wingtip clearance!" }<br>],<br>"instruction": "A nighttime airport apron; an airliner is running its final ground checks before departure",<br>"response_format": "mp3"<br>}'<br>```<br>## Limits and Compatibility<br>* The `instruction` and `roles` fields are limited to 500 characters, and the `scripts` field to 1,000 characters.<br>* `stepaudio-3-gen-preview` does not support the `voice`, `voice_label`, or `timestamp` parameters.<br>* The endpoint returns HTTP 400 when a length limit is exceeded or an unsupported parameter is passed.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

Token count varies by the image.</li><li>* Video message `object`</li><li>Always `video_url`</li><li>* `video_url` `object`</li><li>URL of the video. Format: video/mp4; only http and https are supported. Video must be under 128 MB and the duration is recommended to be under 5 minutes.</li><li>* Audio message `object`</li><li>Always `input_audio`</li><li>* `input_audio` `object`</li><li>Audio content, provided as a base64-encoded audio file.\</li><li>Example base64 format: `data:audio/mpeg;base64,${base64_string}`. Replace the audio format (currently only mp3 and wav are supported) and its corresponding base64 string.</li><li>* `tool_call_id` `string`\</li><li>List of functions supported for Toolcall</li><li>* `reasoning_format` `string` ***optional***\</li><li>Used to instruct the model which reasoning field to use when outputting; default is `general`, meaning general reasoning that returns a `reasoning` field. Options are \[`general`,`deepseek-style`]. When set to `deepseek-style`, you can use the DeepSeek-compatible `reasoning_content` field to obtain the reasoning content.</li><li>* `reasoning_effort` `string` ***optional***\</li><li>Controls how much reasoning the model performs. Models that support three reasoning tiers accept `low`, `medium`, `high`; `step-3.5-flash-2603` accepts `low` and `high` only. Higher values produce deeper reasoning but may take longer to respond.</li><li>- `reasoning` `string`\</li><li>Reasoning content (only for step reasoning models).</li><li>- `tool_calls` `object array` ***optional***\</li><li>Toolcall results.</li><li>Toolcall type, always `function`.</li><li>"content": "Hello! StepFun specializes in artificial intelligence and provides AI solutions across NLP, computer vision, and machine learning to help customers improve efficiency and create value across industries."</li><li>Toolcall content.</li><li>&gt; Note: The examples below use `step-5-preview` by default. To control the model's reasoning depth, see the `reasoning_effort` setting in the "Reasoning Effort" example.</li><li>&gt; [tab] Reasoning Effort</li><li>&gt;     "reasoning_effort": "medium",</li><li>&gt;         "content": "Hello! StepFun is a company focused on artificial intelligence technology and is dedicated to developing and providing various AI solutions. Our AI capabilities cover natural language processing, computer vision, and machine learning to help users in many industries improve efficiency and create value.\n\nWe provide a variety of AI products and services, including intelligent customer service, virtual assistants, smart recommendations, and intelligent moderation. These prod</li><li>&gt; [tab] Image Understanding</li><li>&gt; &gt;             "content": "You are an AI chat assistant provided by StepFun. In addition to being good at Chinese, English, and many other languages, you can also describe the content of user-provided images accurately. While keeping user data safe, you can respond quickly and precisely to users' questions and requests. Your answers and suggestions should reject content related to pornography, gambling, drugs, violence, or terrorism.",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                     "text": "Describe this image with elegant language",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                     "type": "image_url",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                     "image_url": {</li><li>&gt; &gt;                 content: "You are an AI chat assistant provided by StepFun. In addition to being good at Chinese, English, and many other languages, you can also describe the content of user-provided images accurately. Your answers and suggestions should reject content related to pornography, gambling, drugs, violence, or terrorism."</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         text: "Describe this image with elegant language"</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         type: "image_url",</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         image_url: {</li><li>&gt; &gt;         "content": "You are an AI chat assistant provided by StepFun. Change as needed, e.g., read from a file.</li><li>&gt; &gt; image_str = base64.b64encode(r.content).decode("ascii")</li><li>&gt; &gt;                         "url": "data:image/webp;base64,%s" % (image_str),</li><li>&gt; &gt; // Load the image into memory for demo purposes only. | excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Chat Completions API<br>Call the Chat Completions API to get the model-generated chat response data.<br>## Endpoint<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/chat/completions`<br>## Request Parameters<br>* `model` `string` ***required***\<br>Name of the model to use, e.g. `step-5-preview`, `step-3.7-flash`, `step-3.5-flash`, `step-3.5-flash-2603`, `stepaudio-3-chat-preview`, or `stepaudio-2.5-chat`.<br>* `messages` `object array` ***required***\<br>List of different categories of messages entered by the user or generated by the model so far<br>* System message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Role name for system messages, always `system`<br>* `content` `string`\<br>Text content of the system message<br>* User message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Role name for user messages, always `user`<br>* `content` `string or object array`\<br>User message content, either a `multipart` message list or a plain text message string<br>* Plain text message `string`<br>* `multipart` message list `object array`\<br>Structured list of mixed image, video, audio, and text messages<br>* Text message `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `text`<br>* `text` `string`\<br>Message text content<br>* Image message `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `image_url`<br>* `image_url` `object`<br>* `url` `string`\<br>Image URL or base64-encoded image. Supported formats: jpg/jpeg, png, webp, static gif; only http and https are allowed.\<br>&amp;#x20;Example base64 format: `data:image/jpeg;base64,${base64_string}`. Replace the image format (jpeg) and the corresponding base64 string as needed.<br>* `detail` `string` ***optional***\<br>Whether to enable detail mode: choose `low`/`high`, default is `low`.<br>&amp;#x20;`low` mode scales the image to a fixed size with a token count around 400.\<br>`high` mode provides more detailed and richer information. It performs better on large images, OCR, or extreme aspect ratios. Token count varies by the image.<br>* Video message `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `video_url`<br>* `video_url` `object`<br>* `url` `string`\<br>URL of the video. Format: video/mp4; only http and https are supported. Video must be under 128 MB and the duration is recommended to be under 5 minutes.<br>* Audio message `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `input_audio`<br>* `input_audio` `object`<br>* `data` `string`\<br>Audio content, provided as a base64-encoded audio file.\<br>Example base64 format: `data:audio/mpeg;base64,${base64_string}`. Replace the audio format (currently only mp3 and wav are supported) and its corresponding base64 string.<br>* Tool function message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Role name for tool messages, always `tool`<br>* `content` `string`\<br>Content returned after executing the function<br>* `tool_call_id` `string`\<br>ID of the executed function, returned by the assistant in the previous turn.<br>* Chat assistant message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Role name for assistant messages, always `assistant`<br>* `content` `string &#124; null`\<br>Text content of the assistant message<br>* `tools` `object array` ***optional***\<br>List of functions supported for Toolcall<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Tool type, always `function`<br>* `function` `object`\<br>Description of the function<br>* `name` `string`\<br>Function name. Only English letters, numbers, and \_- are allowed; recommended to keep it under 64 characters.<br>* `description` `string`\<br>Function description, supports Chinese and English. It tells the model what the function does so the model can judge and choose.<br>* `parameters` `object`\<br>Parameters of the function<br>* `type` `object`\<br>Parameter description, usually object<br>* `properties` `object`\<br>Function parameter definitions, with the key as the parameter name, and `type` and `description` describing the type and details.<br>* `type` `string&#124;number&#124;integer&#124;object&#124;array&#124;boolean`\<br>Parameter type. See [json-schema](https://json-schema.org/understanding-json-schema/reference/type) for reference.<br>* `description` `string`\<br>Parameter description, supports Chinese and English. It tells the model what each parameter means.<br>* `max_tokens` `int` ***optional***\<br>Maximum number of `tokens` to generate for the chat. Default is INF (no limit, determined automatically by the model). The total of input and generated `tokens` is limited by the specified model's maximum context length.<br>* `temperature` `float` ***optional***\<br>Sampling temperature, a number between 0.0 and 2.0. Higher values (e.g., 0.8) make the output more random; lower values (e.g., 0.2) make it more focused and deterministic.<br>* `top_p` `float` ***optional***\<br>Top-p sampling. The model generates `tokens` within the probability mass of top\_p and outputs them.<br>* `n` `int` ***optional***\<br>Controls how many response messages the model generates for each input message. Default is 1; no hard max, but suggested up to 5.<br>* `stream` `bool` ***optional***\<br>Whether to stream the response messages. Default is false.<br>* `stop` `string &#124; string array` ***optional***\<br>Stops generation when encountering any of the stop content. Empty by default.<br>* `frequency_penalty` `float` ***optional***\<br>Default is 0. A number between 0.0 and 1.0. Higher values penalize tokens that have appeared frequently in the generated text, reducing repetition. |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># List cloned voices<br>Use this API to query the cloned voices under the current account. For the built-in system voices, see [Get system voices](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/system-voices.md).<br>### Endpoint<br>`GET https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/voices`<br>### Request parameters<br>All parameters are query parameters.<br>* `limit` `integer` ***optional***\<br>Page size. Default 20, min 1, max 100. A value outside this range returns `400`.<br>* `order` `string` ***optional***\<br>Sort order by creation time, `asc` or `desc` (default).\<br>&amp;#x20;`asc`: older voices first \<br>&amp;#x20;`desc`: newer voices first<br>* `before` `string` ***optional***\<br>&amp;#x20;Cursor voice ID. Returns the page of voices that precede this ID in the current sort order.<br>* `after` `string` ***optional***\<br>&amp;#x20;Cursor voice ID. Returns the page of voices that follow this ID in the current sort order.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; `before` / `after` must be a voice ID that belongs to your account; otherwise the request fails with `400 voice ... not found or does not belong to this user`. Combine a cursor with `limit` and `order` to page through results, and use `last_id` from the previous response as the next `after` value.<br>### Response<br>* `object` `string`\<br>Always `list`.<br>* `data` `array`\<br>&amp;#x20;Voice entries:<br>* `id` `string` \<br>&amp;#x20;Voice ID for audio generation.<br>* `file_id` `string`\<br>&amp;#x20;Source audio File ID used for cloning.<br>* `created_at` `integer`\<br>&amp;#x20;Creation time, as a Unix timestamp in seconds.<br>* `has_more` `boolean`\<br>&amp;#x20;Whether more pages exist.<br>* `first_id` `string`\<br>&amp;#x20;ID of the first voice in the page.<br>* `last_id` `string`\<br>&amp;#x20;ID of the last voice in the page.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; The response does not indicate which model a voice was cloned with. Keep track of the model you used when creating each voice — see [Clone a voice](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/create-voice.md).<br>### Example<br>&gt; [tab] curl<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; curl --location 'https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/voices' \<br>&gt;   --header 'Authorization: Bearer YOUR_STEP_API_KEY'<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] python<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; import requests<br>&gt;<br>&gt; url = "https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/voices"<br>&gt;<br>&gt; payload = {}<br>&gt; headers = {<br>&gt;     "Authorization": "Bearer YOUR_STEP_API_KEY"<br>&gt; }<br>&gt;<br>&gt; response = requests.request("GET", url, headers=headers, data=payload)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; print(response.text)<br>&gt; ```<br>**Response**<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"object": "list",<br>"data": [<br>{<br>"id": "voice-tone-FmBrMBqicC",<br>"file_id": "file-FmBrMBqicC",<br>"created_at": 1742374363<br>}<br>],<br>"has_more": false,<br>"first_id": "voice-tone-FmBrMBqicC",<br>"last_id": "voice-tone-FmBrMBqicC"<br>}<br>```<br>### Pagination example<br>Request the oldest voice first, then use `last_id` as the cursor for the next page.<br>```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>curl --location 'https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/voices?limit=1&amp;order=asc' \<br>--header 'Authorization: Bearer YOUR_STEP_API_KEY'<br>curl --location 'https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/voices?limit=1&amp;order=asc&amp;after=voice-tone-FmBrMBqicC' \<br>--header 'Authorization: Bearer YOUR_STEP_API_KEY'<br>```<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • ## Reasoning models<br>• &gt; Reasoning models like `step-5-preview` are designed for tasks requiring deep logical analysis, multi-step problem solving, and long-context reasoning.<br>• **Reasoning models excel at:**<br>• * **Long-context Agents**: Maintaining stability and reasoning over massive datasets.<br>• See [Reasoning Model](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/reasoning) for model details.<br>• ## Audio models<br>• &gt; Audio models such as `stepaudio-2.5-tts` convert text into natural speech and support voice cloning.<br>• **Audio models can be used for tasks including, but not limited to:**<br>• * **Audiobooks and podcasts**: text narration with consistent voice.<br>• See [Audio Models](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/audio.md) for model details and [Generate audio](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/create-audio.md) for the API. |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Core Concepts<br>## Reasoning models<br>&gt; Reasoning models like `step-5-preview` are designed for tasks requiring deep logical analysis, multi-step problem solving, and long-context reasoning.<br>**Reasoning models excel at:**<br>* **Complex Logic**: Breaking down intricate problems into manageable steps.<br>* **Mathematics &amp; Coding**: Solving advanced equations and debugging software.<br>* **Long-context Agents**: Maintaining stability and reasoning over massive datasets.<br>See [Reasoning Model](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/reasoning) for model details.<br>## Audio models<br>&gt; Audio models such as `stepaudio-2.5-tts` convert text into natural speech and support voice cloning.<br>**Audio models can be used for tasks including, but not limited to:**<br>* **Voice assistants**: customer service and smart speakers.<br>* **Audiobooks and podcasts**: text narration with consistent voice.<br>* **Games and NPCs**: character voices at scale.<br>* **Media production**: quick voiceover drafts.<br>See [Audio Models](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/audio.md) for model details and [Generate audio](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/create-audio.md) for the API.<br>## Context length<br>&gt; **Context length** is the amount of input text a model considers when generating or predicting. It limits how much information the model processes in a single request.<br>**Why it matters**<br>* **Quality**: Context length governs how much the model can remember and use, affecting understanding and generation.<br>* **Performance**: Larger contexts can improve accuracy but also increase compute cost.<br>* **Cost**: Longer contexts may help in certain scenarios but raise usage costs, so balance quality and spend.<br>**Where it applies**<br>* **Chat systems**: affects coherence and context retention across turns.<br>* **Creative writing**: longer contexts can produce more coherent, logical narratives.<br>* **Research papers**: helps the model digest background, data, and detailed discussion.<br>* **Novels and literature**: captures plot progression and character relationships.<br>## Token<br>&gt; A token is the basic unit of text a model processes. It can be a character, word, phrase, or sentence depending on the tokenizer and training data. In Chinese, tokenization is especially important because words are not separated by spaces.<br>**Token length**<br>* **Chinese characters vs. tokens**: Roughly 1 token equals about 1.5–2 Chinese characters, though actual counts vary by content.<br>**Context limits**<br>* **Maximum context**: The combined input (prompt) and output must stay within the model’s context window.<br>* **Why the limit matters**: It keeps processing efficient and avoids errors from overly long text.<br>**Practical considerations**<br>* **Plan text length**: Fit your text within the maximum context so the model can process everything.<br>* **Optimize tokens**: Remove unnecessary tokens or reorganize text to stay within the limit.<br>## Rate limits<br>&gt; Rate limits protect service stability and fairness by capping how many requests a user can make within a given time. Three main measures:<br>**RPM (requests per minute)**: Number of requests allowed per minute. If RPM is 20, you can make at most 20 requests in any rolling one-minute window.<br>**TPM (tokens per minute)**: Number of tokens you can send per minute across requests and responses. Many short requests may hit RPM before TPM.<br>**Concurrency (simultaneous requests)**: Number of in-flight requests. If the limit is 20, only 20 concurrent requests are allowed; new ones are rejected until earlier ones finish.<br>**When rate limits trigger**: Any one of the above can hit first. For example:<br>```text theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>If your limits are RPM=20 and TPM=200K, and you send 20 requests to ChatCompletions with 100 tokens each,<br>TPM is still under 200K, but hitting 20 requests triggers the RPM limit.<br>```<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Error Codes<br>This guide lists the error codes you may encounter from the API and describes their causes and recommended fixes.<br>## Common Error Codes<br>&#124; Code &#124; Reason &#124; Resolution &#124;<br>&#124; :-: &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; 400 &#124; Request parameters are invalid. Common causes:  1. Image cannot be downloaded  2. Image count exceeds the limit  3. The model does not support video input  4. Model does not exist or you lack permission  5. Parameter value is invalid &#124; Check the docs and pass the parameters correctly. &#124;<br>&#124; 401 &#124; Invalid authentication &#124; Make sure you are using the correct API key. &#124;<br>&#124; 402 &#124; Insufficient balance &#124; Add funds to your account. &#124;<br>&#124; 404 &#124; Incorrect request path &#124; Update the request path per the docs. &#124;<br>&#124; 429 &#124; Resource or rate limit exceeded, usually because requests are too frequent &#124; Retry after a short delay. &#124;<br>&#124; 451 &#124; The request or response content failed review &#124; Modify the request and try again. &#124;<br>&#124; 500 &#124; Server-side issue on our end &#124; Retry shortly; contact us if it persists. &#124;<br>&#124; 503 &#124; Server is currently overloaded &#124; Retry after a short delay. &#124;<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Retrieve file content<br>Retrieve the parsed content of a file. Only files with `purpose=file-extract` are supported.<br>## Endpoint<br>`GET https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/files/{file_id}/content`<br>## Path parameters<br>* `file_id` `string` ***required***<br>Unique file ID.<br>## Response<br>Returns the file's parsed content as plain text.<br>## Example<br>&gt; [tab] python<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; from openai import OpenAI<br>&gt;<br>&gt; client = OpenAI(api_key="YOUR_STEP_API_KEY", base_url="https://api.stepfun.ai/v1")<br>&gt;<br>&gt; content = client.files.content("file-abc123")<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] js<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```js theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; import OpenAI from "openai";<br>&gt;<br>&gt; const openai = new OpenAI({<br>&gt;     apiKey: "YOUR_STEP_API_KEY",<br>&gt;     baseURL: "https://api.stepfun.ai/v1"<br>&gt; });<br>&gt;<br>&gt; async function main() {<br>&gt;     const file = await openai.files.content("file-abc123");<br>&gt;<br>&gt;     console.log(file);<br>&gt; }<br>&gt;<br>&gt; main();<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] curl<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; curl https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/files/file-abc123/content \<br>&gt;   -H "Authorization: Bearer $STEP_API_KEY"<br>&gt; ```<br>```text filename="Response" theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>File content<br>```<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

For voice cloning, audio length should be 5–10 seconds.<br>• &gt;     file=open("productImage.png", "rb"),<br>• &gt;         file: fs.createReadStream("productImage.png"),<br>• &gt;   -F file="@productImage.png"<br>• "filename": "productImage.png", |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Upload a file<br>Upload a file to the file service.<br>## Endpoint<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/files`<br>## Request body<br>* `purpose` `string` ***required***\<br>Upload intent. Only `storage` is supported for file uploads (image/video understanding, voice cloning, etc.).<br>* `url` `string` ***optional***\<br>&amp;#x20;Remote file URL. Supported formats match the `file` field. If both `file` and `url` are provided, `file` takes precedence.<br>* `file` `File` ***optional***\<br>&amp;#x20;File to upload. Each user can upload up to 1,000 files. Omit when using `url`.\<br>`storage` supported formats (max 128 MB):<br>* Video (mp4)<br>* Images (jpg/jpeg, png, webp, static gif)<br>* Audio (mp3, wav). For voice cloning, audio length should be 5–10 seconds.<br>## Response<br>Returns a single [File object](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/files/object.md).<br>## Examples<br>&gt; [tab] python<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; from openai import OpenAI<br>&gt;<br>&gt; client = OpenAI(api_key="YOUR_STEP_API_KEY", base_url="https://api.stepfun.ai/v1")<br>&gt;<br>&gt; client.files.create(<br>&gt;     file=open("productImage.png", "rb"),<br>&gt;     purpose="storage"<br>&gt; )<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] js<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```js theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; import OpenAI from "openai";<br>&gt; import fs from "fs";<br>&gt;<br>&gt; const openai = new OpenAI({<br>&gt;     apiKey: "YOUR_STEP_API_KEY",<br>&gt;     baseURL: "https://api.stepfun.ai/v1"<br>&gt; });<br>&gt;<br>&gt; async function main() {<br>&gt;     const file = await openai.files.create({<br>&gt;         file: fs.createReadStream("productImage.png"),<br>&gt;         purpose: "storage"<br>&gt;     });<br>&gt;<br>&gt;     console.log(file);<br>&gt; }<br>&gt;<br>&gt; main();<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] curl<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; curl https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/files \<br>&gt;   -H "Authorization: Bearer $STEP_API_KEY" \<br>&gt;   -F purpose="storage" \<br>&gt;   -F file="@productImage.png"<br>&gt; ```<br>```json filename="Response" theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"id": "file-abc123",<br>"object": "file",<br>"bytes": 140,<br>"created_at": 1613779121,<br>"filename": "productImage.png",<br>"purpose": "storage",<br>"status": "processed"<br>}<br>```<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |
