Either a string, or an object `{ "url": "...", "detail": "low" }`. The video URL must be publicly reachable from the server.</li><li>&gt; Image and video URLs must be publicly reachable from the server; if the server cannot fetch the URL, a parameter error will be returned. For image input, base64 data URLs are recommended to avoid authentication, hotlink protection, or network access failures on external URLs.</li><li>&gt; `max_output_tokens` limits both the reasoning process and the final output. When using `medium` / `high` reasoning effort, JSON Schema, video, or other complex inputs, reserve a larger output budget; if the budget is insufficient, the response may return `status="incomplete"`, and `output` may contain only a `reasoning` item without a final `message`.</li><li>* `reasoning` `object` ***optional***\</li><li>Reasoning configuration.</li><li>Reasoning effort level. Valid values: `low` / `medium` / `high`.</li><li>Tool-call strategy. may be `completed` upon streaming completion.</li><li>Reasoning summary list; usually an empty array.</li><li>Reasoning content; usually `null`.</li><li>Encrypted reasoning content; usually `null`.</li><li>* `reasoning_tokens` `int`\</li><li>Output tokens used for reasoning.</li><li>* `reasoning` `object or null`\</li><li>Echoes the reasoning configuration.</li><li>Echoes the tool-call strategy.</li><li>"type": "reasoning",</li><li>"output_tokens_details": { "reasoning_tokens": 0, "tool_output_tokens": 0 },</li><li>"reasoning": { "effort": "medium", "summary": null },</li><li>&#124; `response.reasoning_part.added` &#124; A reasoning content part begins &#124;</li><li>&#124; `response.reasoning_text.delta` &#124; Reasoning text delta &#124;</li><li>&#124; `response.reasoning_text.done` &#124; Reasoning text finishes &#124;</li><li>&#124; `response.reasoning_part.done` &#124; Reasoning content part finishes &#124;</li><li>data: {"type":"response.output_item.added","sequence_number":2,"output_index":0,"item":{"id":"rs_xxx","type":"reasoning","summary":[],"content":null,"encrypted_content":null,"status":"in_progress"}}</li><li>event: response.reasoning_part.added</li><li>data: {"type":"response.reasoning_part.added","sequence_number":3,"output_index":0,"item_id":"rs_xxx","content_index":0,"part":{"type":"reasoning_text","text":""}}</li><li>event: response.reasoning_text.delta</li><li>data: {"type":"response.reasoning_text.delta","sequence_number":4,"output_index":0,"item_id":"rs_xxx","content_index":0,"delta":"User asked for a greeting."}</li><li>event: response.reasoning_text.done</li><li>data: {"type":"response.reasoning_text.done","sequence_number":5,"output_index":0,"item_id":"rs_xxx","content_index":0,"text":"User asked for a greeting."}</li><li>event: response.reasoning_part.done</li><li>data: {"type":"response.reasoning_part.done","sequence_number":6,"output_index":0,"item_id":"rs_xxx","content_index":0,"part":{"type":"reasoning_text","text":"User asked for a greeting."}}</li><li>data: {"type":"response.output_item.done","sequence_number":7,"output_index":0,"item":{"id":"rs_xxx","type":"reasoning","summary":[],"content":null,"encrypted_content":null,"status":"completed"}}</li><li>data: {"type":"response.completed","sequence_number":14,"response":{"id":"resp_xxx","object":"response","status":"completed","output":[{"id":"rs_xxx","type":"reasoning","summary":[],"content":null,"encrypted_content":null,"status":"completed"},{"id":"msg_xxx","type":"message","role":"assistant","status":"completed","content":[{"type":"output_text","text":"Hello","annotations":[]}]}],"usage":{"input_tokens":10,"output_tokens":2,"total_tokens":12}}}</li><li>&gt; [tab] Multimodal image</li><li>&gt; &gt; # Load the image into memory — demo only; adapt to reading from disk as needed</li><li>&gt; &gt; image_data_url = "data:image/webp;base64," + base64.b64encode(r.content).decode("ascii")</li><li>&gt; &gt;             {"type": "input_text", "text": "Describe the content of this image"},</li><li>&gt; &gt;             {"type": "input_image", "image_url": image_data_url},</li><li>&gt; &gt; // Load the image into memory — demo only; adapt to reading from disk as needed</li><li>&gt; &gt; async function loadImage(url) {</li><li>&gt; &gt; const imageDataUrl = await loadImage("https://www.stepfun.com/assets/section-1-CTe4nZiO.webp");</li><li>&gt; &gt;             { type: "input_text", text: "Describe the content of this image" },</li><li>&gt; &gt;             { type: "input_image", image_url: imageDataUrl }</li><li>&gt; &gt; image_base64="data:image/webp;base64,"$(curl -s "https://www.stepfun.com/assets/section-1-CTe4nZiO.webp" &#124; base64)</li><li>&gt; &gt;           { \"type\": \"input_text\", \"text\": \"Describe the content of this image\" },</li><li>&gt; &gt;           { \"type\": \"input_image\", \"image_url\": \"${image_base64}\" }</li><li>&gt;   "type": "input_image",</li><li>&gt;   "image_url": { "url": "data:image/jpeg;base64,...", "detail": "high" }</li><li>&gt; [tab] Multimodal video</li><li>&gt; Video is passed via URL; the URL must be publicly reachable from the server:</li><li>&gt; &gt;             {"type": "input_text", "text": "Summarize the main content of this video"},</li><li>&gt; &gt;             {"type": "input_video", "video_url": {"url": "https://example.com/demo.mp4", "detail": "low"}},</li><li>&gt; &gt;             { type: "input_text", text: "Summarize the main content of this video" },</li><li>&gt; &gt;             { type: "input_video", video_url: { url: "https://example.com/demo.mp4", detail: "low" } }</li><li>&gt; &gt;           { "type": "input_text", "text": "Summarize the main content of this video" },</li><li>&gt; &gt;           { "type": "input_video", "video_url": {"url": "https://example.com/demo.mp4", "detail": "low"} }</li><li>&gt; [tab] Structured output</li><li>&gt; [tab] Reasoning effort</li><li>&gt; &gt;     reasoning={"effort": "high"},</li><li>&gt; &gt;     reasoning: { effort: "high" }</li><li>&gt; &gt;     "reasoning": { "effort": "high" }</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Responses API<br>Call the Responses API to get the model-generated response data. This endpoint is compatible with the OpenAI Responses API request and response format.<br>## Endpoint<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/responses`<br>## Request Parameters<br>* `model` `string` ***required***\<br>Name of the model to use. Currently supported models are `step-5-preview` and `step-3.7-flash`.<br>* `input` `string or object array` ***required***\<br>Input content. Either a plain text string, or an ordered array of messages / events.<br>* Plain text input `string`\<br>Equivalent to a single text message with `role=user`.<br>* Message / event array `object array`\<br>Each element may be one of the following types:<br>* Text message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Message role. Valid values: `user`, `assistant`, `system`.<br>* `content` `string or object array`\<br>Message content. Either a plain text string, or an array of content blocks for multimodal input.<br>* Plain text content `string`<br>* Content block array `object array`<br>* Text block `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `input_text`.<br>* `text` `string`\<br>Text content.<br>* Image block `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `input_image`.<br>* `image_url` `string or object`\<br>Image source. Either a string, or an object `{ "url": "...", "detail": "high" }`. Base64 data URLs (format `data:image/jpeg;base64,${base64_string}`) are recommended; otherwise the external URL must be publicly reachable from the server.<br>* Video block `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `input_video`.<br>* `video_url` `string or object`\<br>Video source. Either a string, or an object `{ "url": "...", "detail": "low" }`. The video URL must be publicly reachable from the server.<br>* Function call `object` (returned by the model in a previous turn; must be passed back verbatim in multi-turn conversations)<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `function_call`.<br>* `id` `string`\<br>Unique ID for the function call, generated by the server.<br>* `call_id` `string`\<br>Correlation ID used to pair with the subsequent `function_call_output`.<br>* `name` `string`\<br>Tool name.<br>* `arguments` `string`\<br>Arguments as a JSON string.<br>* Function call output `object` (returned by the client after executing the tool)<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `function_call_output`.<br>* `call_id` `string`\<br>The `call_id` of the matching `function_call`.<br>* `output` `string`\<br>Tool execution result (JSON string recommended).<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Image and video URLs must be publicly reachable from the server; if the server cannot fetch the URL, a parameter error will be returned. For image input, base64 data URLs are recommended to avoid authentication, hotlink protection, or network access failures on external URLs.<br>* `instructions` `string` ***optional***\<br>Top-level system instructions.<br>* `stream` `bool` ***optional***\<br>Whether to enable SSE streaming. Default is `false`.<br>* `temperature` `float` ***optional***\<br>Sampling temperature, between 0.0 and 2.0.<br>* `top_p` `float` ***optional***\<br>Nucleus sampling parameter.<br>* `max_output_tokens` `int` ***optional***\<br>Maximum number of output tokens for this response.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; `max_output_tokens` limits both the reasoning process and the final output. When using `medium` / `high` reasoning effort, JSON Schema, video, or other complex inputs, reserve a larger output budget; if the budget is insufficient, the response may return `status="incomplete"`, and `output` may contain only a `reasoning` item without a final `message`.<br>* `reasoning` `object` ***optional***\<br>Reasoning configuration.<br>* `effort` `string` ***optional***\<br>Reasoning effort level. Valid values: `low` / `medium` / `high`.<br>* `tools` `object array` ***optional***\<br>List of tool definitions. Currently only `function`-type tools are supported.<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `function`.<br>* `name` `string`\<br>Function name. Letters, digits, and `_-` are recommended.<br>* `description` `string` ***optional***\<br>Function description — helps the model decide whether to call the tool.<br>* `parameters` `object`\<br>JSON Schema describing the function arguments.<br>* `strict` `boolean` ***optional***\<br>Whether to enable strict mode. When enabled, the model's output arguments will strictly match the schema in `parameters`.<br>* `tool_choice` `string or object` ***optional***\<br>Tool-call strategy. Currently only the string `"auto"` is supported (the model decides whether to call a tool).<br>* `text` `object` ***optional***\<br>Text output format configuration.<br>* `format` `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>One of `text`, `json_object`, or `json_schema`.<br>* `name` `string` ***optional***\<br>Required when `type=json_schema`. The schema's identifier name.<br>* `strict` `boolean` ***optional***\<br>Available when `type=json_schema`. When enabled, the model's output will strictly match the schema.<br>* `schema` `object` ***optional***\ |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Step Plan Overview<br>Related resources: [Step Code authentication](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-code/configuration/models.md) · [Pay-as-you-go pricing and rate limits](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/pricing/details.md).<br>Step Plan is a subscription service from the StepFun API Platform that lets you use StepFun models at a subscription price in mainstream coding tools and agent platforms such as OpenClaw, Claude Code, Trae, and Cursor. After subscribing, you connect with a valid Step API Key and receive a unified model usage allowance every month.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Step Plan has moved to **Credit-based monthly allowance** billing: your allowance is issued in Credits each month and can be spent flexibly within the month. If you subscribed to the earlier request-based plan, see the [Step Plan upgrade notice](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/upgrade-notice.md) for what changed and how to upgrade.<br>## Key features<br>* **One billing unit for everything**: usage across all models is measured in Credits, so there is no need to track separate prices per model.<br>* **Fast model experience by default**: every tier provides the same high-speed model performance, with no extra charge for speed.<br>* **Monthly allowance, flexible spending**: Credits are issued once per month and can be spent at any time within the month, with no time-window or request-frequency limits.<br>* **Top up when you run out**: if you use up your monthly allowance, you can purchase booster packs instead of waiting for the next month.<br>* **Available across platforms**: no platform restrictions. One subscription covers mainstream coding and agent toolchains.<br>* **Multiple model families**: covers text, reasoning, and speech models, with more on the way.<br>## Billing<br>Step Plan uses **Credits** as its unified billing unit. When you call any model, the usage is converted into Credits and deducted from your current month's allowance.<br>Credits convert to payment at approximately **\$1 ≈ 7M Credits** (7 million Credits correspond to about \$1 of model usage). What differs between tiers is the number of Credits issued each month.<br>After you subscribe, the full month's Credits are issued to your monthly allowance at once. They can be spent at any time within the month, are cleared at the end of the month, and do not roll over to the next cycle.<br>### Deduction order<br>When your account holds both monthly-allowance Credits and booster-pack Credits, usage is deducted from whichever expires first. Monthly-allowance Credits expire when the month's allowance is cleared; booster-pack Credits expire on their own 30-day expiration dates.<br>## Plan tiers<br>Step Plan offers four tiers. They differ in the monthly Credit allowance and price; all tiers provide the same high-speed model performance.<br>&#124; Plan &#124; Best for &#124; Monthly Credits &#124; Monthly &#124; Quarterly &#124; Yearly &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; **Flash Mini** &#124; Getting started with AI &#124; 400M &#124; \$6.99 &#124; \$18.99 &#124; \$69.99 &#124;<br>&#124; **Flash Plus** &#124; Daily productivity &#124; 1,600M &#124; \$9.99 &#124; \$26.99 &#124; \$95.99 &#124;<br>&#124; **Flash Pro** &#124; Heavy AI use &#124; 8,000M &#124; \$29 &#124; \$79 &#124; \$289 &#124;<br>&#124; **Flash Max** &#124; Professional workloads &#124; 40,000M &#124; \$99 &#124; \$269 &#124; \$989 &#124;<br>Quarterly and yearly plans are paid once for the full period, but Credits are still issued month by month (quarterly = monthly Credits × 3 months, yearly = monthly Credits × 12 months). "M" stands for million Credits.<br>Every tier includes: access to all flagship models, coverage of multiple MCP tools, and multi-device login. Flash Plus and above also include priority API rates and priority technical support.<br>## Booster packs<br>When your monthly allowance runs out, you can purchase booster packs to add Credits without waiting for the next month's issuance. Booster packs are available only to active Step Plan subscribers, run on their own separate 30-day cycles, and are independent of your subscription's expiration date.<br>&#124; Booster pack &#124; Price &#124; Credits &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; Small &#124; \$6.99 &#124; 400M &#124;<br>&#124; Large &#124; \$9.99 &#124; 1,600M &#124;<br>## Supported models<br>Step Plan currently supports the following models, covering text, reasoning, and speech. For each model's capabilities and parameters, see the [model capability overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/overview.md).<br>* [`step-5-preview`](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/step-5-preview): flagship model for agentic work<br>* [`step-3.7-flash`](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/step-3.7-flash): high-speed multimodal reasoning model<br>* [`step-3.5-flash`](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/step-3.5-flash): high-speed model for agent and coding tasks<br>* `step-3.5-flash-2603`: a version optimized for high-frequency agent scenarios<br>* [`stepaudio-2.5-tts`](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/stepaudio-2.5-tts): next-generation contextual TTS model<br>* [`stepaudio-2.5-asr`](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/stepaudio-2.5-asr): next-generation automatic speech recognition model<br>More StepFun flagship models will be added over time.<br>## Getting connected<br>After you have a valid Step API Key, choose the Step Plan configuration URL for your tool:<br>* **Claude Code / Anthropic SDK:** `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan`<br>* **OpenAI SDK Chat Completions calls:** `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1`<br>These are client configuration URLs (Base URL), not the full HTTP request URLs. See the [Messages API](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/chat/messages-create.md) and the matching API reference for full request URLs. For Claude Code setup, see the [Claude Code integration guide](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/claude-code.md). For other tools, follow the tool-specific [integration guide](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/quick-start.md).<br>The Step Plan channel consumes subscription Credits. The standard API channel has a separate quota. Requests sent to the standard API address enter the standard API channel; whether they succeed depends on that channel's permissions and account status. A successful call does not mean Step Plan Credits were consumed.<br>## Frequently asked questions<br>&gt; [accordion] How is Step Plan different from calling the API directly?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Step Plan is a subscription: its monthly Credit allowance provides more usage than pay-as-you-go at the same spend, and Credits are issued monthly and can be spent at any time within the month, which makes costs easier to control. The two use different Base URLs and do not affect each other.<br>&gt; [accordion] How are Credits billed?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Credits are Step Plan's unified billing unit; each model's usage is converted into Credits and deducted. Credits convert at approximately \$1 ≈ 7M Credits. After you subscribe, the full month's Credits are issued at once, to be spent within the month and cleared at month end.<br>&gt; [accordion] Do unused monthly Credits roll over to the next month?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; No. Monthly-allowance Credits are cleared at the end of the month and do not carry over to the next cycle. Booster packs run on their own separate 30-day cycles and expire on their own dates.<br>&gt; [accordion] What happens when I use up this month's Credits?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; You can purchase booster packs to add Credits (Small at \$6.99 / 400M, Large at \$9.99 / 1600M, each on its own 30-day cycle), or wait for the next month's allowance to be issued.<br>&gt; [accordion] Is Step Plan subject to tiered rate limits (RPM / TPM)?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; No. Step Plan is not subject to the platform's [tiered rate limits](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/pricing/details.md#tiered-rate-limits) based on cumulative top-up amounts. Your usage is governed by the monthly Credit allowance of your subscribed tier.<br>&gt; [accordion] What is the relationship between Step Plan and my account balance?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; They are two separate systems. Usage within Step Plan draws on the subscription's own Credit allowance and does not deduct from your account balance. You can, however, use your account balance (top-up funds) to pay for a Step Plan subscription.<br>&gt; [accordion] Which payment methods are supported?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Subscriptions can be paid through Stripe, or redeemed using your account balance (top-up funds).<br>&gt; [accordion] Can I cancel my subscription?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Yes, at any time. Fees already paid are not refunded, and service continues until the end of the current billing cycle.<br>&gt; [accordion] Which Base URL does Step Plan use?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Choose by tool: Claude Code / Anthropic SDK uses `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan`; OpenAI SDK Chat Completions calls use `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1`. In Claude Code, do not paste the full `/v1/messages` request URL into the Base URL. See the [Claude Code integration guide](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/claude-code.md) and the [Messages API](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/chat/messages-create.md).<br>&gt; [accordion] What should I do if a third-party tool throws errors?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; First confirm the Base URL for that tool, your current model permissions, and your Step Plan subscription and Credit status. If you previously connected another provider, check old URL, model, or key settings, then fully restart the client. If it still fails, share the client version, error message, time of the issue, and a request ID if you have one. Do not send the full API Key.<br>&gt; [accordion] Will more models be added in the future?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Yes. Step Plan will gradually expand to more existing and future StepFun flagship models.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| plan or price mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（39 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li># Step Plan Overview</li><li>Step Plan is a subscription service from the StepFun API Platform that lets you use StepFun models at a subscription price in mainstream coding tools and agent platforms such as OpenClaw, Claude Code, Trae, and Cursor. After subscribing, you connect with a valid Step API Key and receive a unified model usage allowance every month.</li><li>&gt; Step Plan has moved to **Credit-based monthly allowance** billing: your allowance is issued in Credits each month and can be spent flexibly within the month. If you subscribed to the earlier request-based plan, see the [Step Plan upgrade notice](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/upgrade-notice.md) for what changed and how to upgrade.</li><li>* **Monthly allowance, flexible spending**: Credits are issued once per month and can be spent at any time within the month, with no time-window or request-frequency limits.</li><li>* **Top up when you run out**: if you use up your monthly allowance, you can purchase booster packs instead of waiting for the next month.</li><li>* **Available across platforms**: no platform restrictions. One subscription covers mainstream coding and agent toolchains.</li><li>Step Plan uses **Credits** as its unified billing unit. When you call any model, the usage is converted into Credits and deducted from your current month's allowance.</li><li>Credits convert to payment at approximately **\$1 ≈ 7M Credits** (7 million Credits correspond to about \$1 of model usage). What differs between tiers is the number of Credits issued each month.</li><li>After you subscribe, the full month's Credits are issued to your monthly allowance at once. They can be spent at any time within the month, are cleared at the end of the month, and do not roll over to the next cycle.</li><li>When your account holds both monthly-allowance Credits and booster-pack Credits, usage is deducted from whichever expires first. Monthly-allowance Credits expire when the month's allowance is cleared; booster-pack Credits expire on their own 30-day expiration dates.</li><li>## Plan tiers</li><li>Step Plan offers four tiers. They differ in the monthly Credit allowance and price; all tiers provide the same high-speed model performance.</li><li>&#124; Plan &#124; Best for &#124; Monthly Credits &#124; Monthly &#124; Quarterly &#124; Yearly &#124;</li><li>&#124; **Flash Mini** &#124; Getting started with AI &#124; 400M &#124; \$6.99 &#124; \$18.99 &#124; \$69.99 &#124;</li><li>&#124; **Flash Plus** &#124; Daily productivity &#124; 1,600M &#124; \$9.99 &#124; \$26.99 &#124; \$95.99 &#124;</li><li>&#124; **Flash Pro** &#124; Heavy AI use &#124; 8,000M &#124; \$29 &#124; \$79 &#124; \$289 &#124;</li><li>&#124; **Flash Max** &#124; Professional workloads &#124; 40,000M &#124; \$99 &#124; \$269 &#124; \$989 &#124;</li><li>Quarterly and yearly plans are paid once for the full period, but Credits are still issued month by month (quarterly = monthly Credits × 3 months, yearly = monthly Credits × 12 months). "M" stands for million Credits.</li><li>When your monthly allowance runs out, you can purchase booster packs to add Credits without waiting for the next month's issuance. Booster packs are available only to active Step Plan subscribers, run on their own separate 30-day cycles, and are independent of your subscription's expiration date.</li><li>&#124; Small &#124; \$6.99 &#124; 400M &#124;</li><li>&#124; Large &#124; \$9.99 &#124; 1,600M &#124;</li><li>Step Plan currently supports the following models, covering text, reasoning, and speech. For each model's capabilities and parameters, see the [model capability overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/models/overview.md).</li><li>After you have a valid Step API Key, choose the Step Plan configuration URL for your tool:</li><li>These are client configuration URLs (Base URL), not the full HTTP request URLs. See the [Messages API](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/chat/messages-create.md) and the matching API reference for full request URLs. For Claude Code setup, see the [Claude Code integration guide](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/claude-code.md). For other tools, follow the tool-specific [integration guide](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/quick-start.md</li><li>The Step Plan channel consumes subscription Credits. The standard API channel has a separate quota. Requests sent to the standard API address enter the standard API channel; whether they succeed depends on that channel's permissions and account status. A successful call does not mean Step Plan Credits were consumed.</li><li>&gt; [accordion] How is Step Plan different from calling the API directly?</li><li>&gt; Step Plan is a subscription: its monthly Credit allowance provides more usage than pay-as-you-go at the same spend, and Credits are issued monthly and can be spent at any time within the month, which makes costs easier to control. The two use different Base URLs and do not affect each other.</li><li>&gt; Credits are Step Plan's unified billing unit; each model's usage is converted into Credits and deducted. Credits convert at approximately \$1 ≈ 7M Credits. After you subscribe, the full month's Credits are issued at once, to be spent within the month and cleared at month end.</li><li>&gt; [accordion] Do unused monthly Credits roll over to the next month?</li><li>&gt; No. Monthly-allowance Credits are cleared at the end of the month and do not carry over to the next cycle. Booster packs run on their own separate 30-day cycles and expire on their own dates.</li><li>&gt; You can purchase booster packs to add Credits (Small at \$6.99 / 400M, Large at \$9.99 / 1600M, each on its own 30-day cycle), or wait for the next month's allowance to be issued.</li><li>&gt; [accordion] Is Step Plan subject to tiered rate limits (RPM / TPM)?</li><li>&gt; No. Step Plan is not subject to the platform's [tiered rate limits](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/pricing/details.md#tiered-rate-limits) based on cumulative top-up amounts. Your usage is governed by the monthly Credit allowance of your subscribed tier.</li><li>&gt; [accordion] What is the relationship between Step Plan and my account balance?</li><li>&gt; They are two separate systems. Usage within Step Plan draws on the subscription's own Credit allowance and does not deduct from your account balance. You can, however, use your account balance (top-up funds) to pay for a Step Plan subscription.</li><li>&gt; [accordion] Can I cancel my subscription?</li><li>&gt; [accordion] Which Base URL does Step Plan use?</li><li>&gt; First confirm the Base URL for that tool, your current model permissions, and your Step Plan subscription and Credit status. If you previously connected another provider, check old URL, model, or key settings, then fully restart the client. If it still fails, share the client version, error message, time of the issue, and a request ID if you have one. Do not send the full API Key.</li><li>&gt; Yes. Step Plan will gradually expand to more existing and future StepFun flagship models.</li></ul></details> |

For the Messages API (Anthropic-compatible), use `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan`.<br>• &gt; Note: When manually editing the Claude Code configuration file, set `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` to the Messages API (Anthropic-compatible) base URL: `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan` (without `/v1`). Claude Code automatically appends `/v1/messages` when making requests.<br>• * Whether the `Base URL` matches the tool protocol: use `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1` for the Chat Completions API (OpenAI-compatible) or OpenAI Compatible tools, and `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan` for the Messages API (Anthropic-compatible) or Claude Code configuration files |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Quick Start<br>Step Plan quota can be used with Step Code and third-party AI coding tools.<br>## 1. Prepare Your Account and API Key<br>Step Code signs in with a Step Plan account. This section also prepares the account and API Key that third-party tools need.<br>&gt; Recommended order for third-party tools: confirm the Step Plan subscription, create an API Key, then configure the tool.<br>### Subscribe to a Step Plan<br>If this is your first time using the open platform, it is recommended to first check whether your current account has completed Step Plan subscription or activation. In general, only after you have an available package, plan, or corresponding calling permissions will subsequent model calls, quota usage, and capability usage take effect properly.<br>If your account has already been enabled by your team, you can go directly to the next step. If it has not been enabled yet, it is recommended to first complete the subscription to the corresponding plan on the platform, and then continue to create an API Key and integrate third-party tools.<br>### Obtain an API Key<br>How to obtain it:<br>* [StepFun Open Platform](https://platform.stepfun.ai/interface-key)<br>It is recommended that you create a new API Key in the console and keep it safe. Do not write it directly into your code repository.<br>For your first integration, it is recommended to prioritize using the following model for verification:<br>```text theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>step-5-preview<br>```<br>In different tools, you will frequently see these configuration items:<br>* `API Provider`: Select the interface provider type, such as `OpenAI Compatible`<br>* `Base URL`: Choose the model service address based on the protocol used by the tool. For the Chat Completions API (OpenAI-compatible) or OpenAI Compatible tools, use `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1`. For the Messages API (Anthropic-compatible), use `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan`.<br>* `API Key`: The key obtained from the Step platform<br>* `Model` or `Model ID`: Enter `step-5-preview`<br>&gt; Note: When manually editing the Claude Code configuration file, set `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` to the Messages API (Anthropic-compatible) base URL: `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan` (without `/v1`). Claude Code automatically appends `/v1/messages` when making requests.<br>## 2. Choose an Integration Tool<br>Step Code is StepFun's terminal coding agent: it reads and edits files in a project, runs commands, keeps long-running tasks going, and extends through MCP servers, Agent Skills, subagents, and plugins.<br>* [Step Code Quick Start](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-code/quickstart.md): install the CLI, sign in, and analyze a file.<br>### Third-party tools<br>The tools below call Step models through a Base URL and an API Key. Each tool page covers its own configuration.<br>{stepPlanTools.map((tool) =&gt; (<br>{tool.title}<br>{tool.description}<br>))}<br>### Common Checks<br>For third-party tools, check the following items first when a call fails:<br>* Whether the API Key is entered correctly and belongs to the correct environment<br>* Whether the `Base URL` matches the tool protocol: use `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1` for the Chat Completions API (OpenAI-compatible) or OpenAI Compatible tools, and `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan` for the Messages API (Anthropic-compatible) or Claude Code configuration files<br>* Whether `Model` or `Model ID` is set to `step-5-preview`<br>* Whether the tool has already saved the configuration and been restarted<br>Step Code sign-in and runtime issues are covered in [FAQ and troubleshooting](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-code/reference/faq.md).<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

All requests uniformly use the `/step_plan/v1/...` path prefix, and the domain name is fixed as `https://api.stepfun.ai`.</li><li>&#124; `step-5-preview` &#124; Flagship model for agentic work, with text, image, and video input and a 1M-token context window. &#124;</li><li>&#124; `step-3.7-flash` &#124; High-speed multimodal reasoning model with native image and video understanding. Supports three reasoning effort levels (low/medium/high) — well-suited to agent, coding, and multimodal workloads. &#124;</li><li>## Reasoning Effort</li><li>Models that support three reasoning effort levels accept `low`, `medium`, or `high` in the request. Just set the Base URL to `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1` and select `step-5-preview`, `step-3.7-flash`, `step-3.5-flash-2603`, or `step-3.5-flash` as the model.</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Reasoning Model Integration<br>Step Plan supports accessing StepFun reasoning models via a dedicated path. All requests uniformly use the `/step_plan/v1/...` path prefix, and the domain name is fixed as `https://api.stepfun.ai`.<br>## Prerequisites<br>1. Subscribed to a [Step Plan](https://platform.stepfun.ai/step-plan).<br>2. Obtained an [API Key](https://platform.stepfun.ai/interface-key).<br>***<br>## Supported Models<br>&#124; Model &#124; Description &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `step-5-preview` &#124; Flagship model for agentic work, with text, image, and video input and a 1M-token context window. &#124;<br>&#124; `step-3.7-flash` &#124; High-speed multimodal reasoning model with native image and video understanding. Supports three reasoning effort levels (low/medium/high) — well-suited to agent, coding, and multimodal workloads. &#124;<br>&#124; `step-3.5-flash-2603` &#124; Optimized from Step 3.5 Flash for high-frequency Agent scenarios. Improved token efficiency and faster inference; can switch to low-inference mode to significantly reduce token consumption. &#124;<br>&#124; `step-3.5-flash` &#124; Sparse MoE architecture with 196B total / 11B activated parameters. High-speed inference, optimized for agent and coding tasks. &#124;<br>## Endpoint Paths<br>&#124; Capability &#124; Request Method &#124; Step Plan Path &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; Chat Completion (OpenAI protocol) &#124; POST &#124; `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/chat/completions` &#124;<br>&#124; Messages (Anthropic protocol) &#124; POST &#124; `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/messages` &#124;<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; The endpoint parameters are exactly the same as the open platform. For details, see the [Chat Completion API docs](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/chat/chat-completion-create.md) and the Messages API docs.<br>&gt; [!WARNING]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; The Anthropic SDK automatically appends `/v1/messages` to the base URL, so when using the Anthropic SDK, set the base URL to `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan` (without `/v1`). The OpenAI SDK uses `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1`.<br>## Reasoning Effort<br>Models that support three reasoning effort levels accept `low`, `medium`, or `high` in the request. Chat Completion (OpenAI protocol) uses `reasoning_effort`; Messages (Anthropic protocol) uses `output_config.effort`.<br>&#124; Effort &#124; Recommended Use &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; `low` &#124; Simple Q\&amp;A, summarization, rewriting, information extraction &#124;<br>&#124; `medium` &#124; Default recommendation; suits general reasoning and multi-step tasks &#124;<br>&#124; `high` &#124; Complex reasoning, math, planning, code analysis &#124;<br>## Billing<br>The billing logic is consistent with the open platform. The actual amount billed on the open platform is converted into Step Plan Credit consumption. For details on plan entitlements, see the [Step Plan overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md).<br>## Integration Methods<br>### Direct API Calls<br>&gt; [tab] curl<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; curl -X POST 'https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/chat/completions' \<br>&gt; -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \<br>&gt; -H "Authorization: Bearer $STEP_API_KEY" \<br>&gt; -d '{<br>&gt;     "model": "step-5-preview",<br>&gt;     "messages": [<br>&gt;         {"role": "user", "content": "Hello, please introduce yourself."}<br>&gt;     ]<br>&gt; }'<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] Python (OpenAI SDK)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; from openai import OpenAI<br>&gt;<br>&gt; client = OpenAI(<br>&gt;     api_key="YOUR_STEP_API_KEY",<br>&gt;     base_url="https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1",<br>&gt; )<br>&gt;<br>&gt; response = client.chat.completions.create(<br>&gt;     model="step-5-preview",<br>&gt;     messages=[<br>&gt;         {"role": "user", "content": "Hello, please introduce yourself."}<br>&gt;     ],<br>&gt; )<br>&gt;<br>&gt; print(response.choices[0].message.content)<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] Python (Anthropic SDK)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; from anthropic import Anthropic<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Note: The Anthropic SDK automatically appends /v1/messages; base_url should not include /v1<br>&gt; client = Anthropic(<br>&gt;     api_key="YOUR_STEP_API_KEY",<br>&gt;     base_url="https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan",<br>&gt; )<br>&gt;<br>&gt; message = client.messages.create(<br>&gt;     model="step-5-preview",<br>&gt;     max_tokens=1024,<br>&gt;     messages=[<br>&gt;         {"role": "user", "content": "Hello, please introduce yourself."}<br>&gt;     ],<br>&gt; )<br>&gt;<br>&gt; print(message.content[0].text)<br>&gt; ```<br>### Via Tool Integrations<br>Reasoning models can be integrated through a variety of Agent tools and coding assistants. Just set the Base URL to `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1` and select `step-5-preview`, `step-3.7-flash`, `step-3.5-flash-2603`, or `step-3.5-flash` as the model.<br>See the [Quick Start](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/quick-start.md) and the individual tool integration guides:<br>&gt; [card] [OpenClaw](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/openclaw.md)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Command-driven Agents and initialization-based workflows.<br>&gt; [card] [Claude Code](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/claude-code.md)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Coding, debugging, and engineering collaboration in the terminal. |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Tool Integration Overview<br>Step Plan subscriptions work with Step Code and third-party tools.<br>## Step Code<br>* [Step Code](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-code/configuration/models.md): StepFun's terminal-based AI agent; use subscription login to call Step models with Step Plan quota.<br>## Other Terminal Tools (CLI / TUI)<br>* [Claude Code](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/claude-code.md): Connect Step models through the Messages API, with configurable reasoning effort and a 1M context window.<br>* [Codex CLI](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/codex.md): Connect Step models through a custom provider.<br>* [OpenClaw](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/openclaw.md): Enter the Step Plan endpoint and API key in a custom provider.<br>* [OpenCode](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/open-code.md): Set `OPENAI_BASE_URL` to `https://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1` to call Step models with Step Plan quota.<br>* [Pi](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/pi.md): Connect Step models through the Anthropic Messages protocol; the [Oh My Pi](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/oh-my-pi.md) workflow extension reuses the same model configuration and adds task categories, specialist roles, and skills.<br>* [Goose](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/goose.md): Connect Step models through the OpenAI-compatible API.<br>* [Hermes Agent](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/hermes-agent.md): Select StepFun Step Plan during configuration and add your API key, then work from a terminal TUI or messaging platforms such as Telegram.<br>## Editors and IDEs<br>* [Cursor](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/cursor.md): Turn on Override OpenAI Base URL to call Step models in Chat and Agent.<br>* [Cline](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/cline.md): Select the StepFun Step Plan provider to connect Step models.<br>* [CodeGPT](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/codegpt.md): Connect Step Plan through the Custom provider OpenAI-compatible interface.<br>* [Kilo Code](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/kilo-code.md): Connect Step models through the Step Plan provider.<br>* [Roo Code](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/roo-code.md): Connect Step Plan through the OpenAI Compatible provider.<br>* [Zed](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/zed.md): Configure a custom API endpoint for Step models.<br>## Desktop Clients<br>* [Cherry Studio](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/cherry-studio.md): Add a Step API key and provider to call Step models from the desktop client.<br>* [DeepSeek Harness](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/deepseek-harness.md): DeepSeek's open-source agent harness; add a custom provider in the local Web UI to connect Step 5 Preview.<br>* [WorkBuddy](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/guides/developer/integrations/workbuddy.md): Add a custom OpenAI-compatible model to chat and run file tasks from the desktop client.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

#### 来源 / stepfun-global-step-plan-upgrade

| 字段 | 变化 | 原值 | 新值 |
|---|---|---|---|
| access scope | 新增 | 未记录 | public |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 空列表 |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Step Plan Upgrade Notice<br>As of July 24, 2026, Step Plan has been upgraded to **Credit-based monthly allowance** billing. The new plan is called the **Token Plan**. If you are on the earlier **Coding Plan**, your benefits for the current cycle are not affected. Subscribers with auto-renewal enabled can keep renewing on the Coding Plan; subscribers without auto-renewal need to upgrade to the Token Plan after their current cycle expires to continue using Step Plan. Upgrading to the Token Plan is one-way and cannot be reverted.<br>## What changed<br>The Token Plan upgrades three things: the billing unit, the plan structure, and what happens when you hit your limit.<br>&#124; Change &#124; Old Coding Plan &#124; New Token Plan &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; Billing unit &#124; Per request (Prompt) &#124; Credit (unified billing unit, \$1 ≈ 7M Credits) &#124;<br>&#124; Plan structure &#124; 5-hour / weekly limits &#124; Monthly Credit allowance: issued monthly, spend anytime within the month, cleared at month end &#124;<br>&#124; When you hit the limit &#124; Usage cut off &#124; Purchase booster packs on their own separate 30-day cycles &#124;<br>For the new plan's tier pricing, Credit allowances, booster packs, and billing rules, see the [Step Plan overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md).<br>## For existing Coding Plan subscribers<br>If you are on the earlier Coding Plan, your allowance, benefits, and expiration date for the current cycle remain unchanged, and you can keep using the plan until the cycle ends. Whether you need to upgrade depends on your auto-renewal setting:<br>* **Auto-renewal enabled**: your Coding Plan will keep renewing automatically for the next cycle, and you can continue using it without upgrading. You can also choose to upgrade to the Token Plan at any time. Note that a failed renewal payment will cancel your Coding Plan, and it cannot be resubscribed. You would need to purchase the new Token Plan to keep using Step Plan. To stay on the Coding Plan, make sure your payment method is valid and has sufficient funds before each renewal date.<br>* **Auto-renewal disabled**: the Coding Plan does not support manual renewal. To continue using Step Plan after your current cycle expires, upgrade to the new Token Plan.<br>&gt; [!WARNING]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Upgrading to the Token Plan is one-way and cannot be reverted to the Coding Plan. Once a Coding Plan expires without renewal or is cancelled, it cannot be resubscribed, and continuing on Step Plan requires purchasing the Token Plan. We recommend acting in time to avoid service interruption.<br>## Upgrading to the Token Plan<br>Coding Plan subscribers can upgrade to the new Token Plan from the console. The upgrade works as follows:<br>* **Your expiration date stays the same**: whatever date your plan expired on before, it still expires on after the upgrade.<br>* **Full allowance issued**: the new plan's full monthly Credit allowance is issued immediately on upgrade, not prorated by remaining days. For example, 3.5 months left on the old plan converts to 4 months of the new plan.<br>* **One-way**: once confirmed, the upgrade cannot be reverted to the old plan.<br>* **No tier change during upgrade**: the upgrade switches you from the old plan to the same tier of the new plan. To move to a higher tier, complete the upgrade first and then change tiers (see below).<br>## Changing to a higher tier<br>After upgrading, you can move your Token Plan to a higher tier within the cycle by paying the prorated difference:<br>* **Amount due** = original tier price × (1 − time used / total cycle). The difference is calculated only on the unused portion of the cycle and does not involve converting your remaining Credit balance.<br>* The full month's Credits for the new tier are issued immediately after the tier change.<br>* Constraints: the new tier's price must be no lower than the original tier's price, and the new tier's cycle must be no shorter than your remaining subscription time.<br>The exact amounts and presentation are subject to what the console subscription page shows.<br>## Frequently asked questions<br>&gt; [accordion] Can I keep using the Coding Plan after it expires?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; It depends on your auto-renewal setting. With auto-renewal enabled, your Coding Plan keeps renewing automatically and you can continue using it without upgrading, as long as each renewal payment goes through. With auto-renewal disabled, the Coding Plan does not support manual renewal, so you need to upgrade to the Token Plan after expiration to continue.<br>&gt; [accordion] I have auto-renewal enabled on the Coding Plan. Will I be forced onto the Token Plan?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; No. As long as renewals go through, you keep renewing on the Coding Plan, and whether to upgrade to the Token Plan is entirely up to you. If a renewal payment fails, however, your Coding Plan is cancelled and cannot be resubscribed. You would then need to purchase the Token Plan to continue using Step Plan.<br>&gt; [accordion] What happens if my auto-renewal payment fails?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Your Coding Plan is cancelled. A cancelled Coding Plan cannot be resubscribed, so you would need to purchase the new Token Plan to keep using Step Plan. We recommend checking in advance that your payment method is valid and has sufficient funds.<br>&gt; [accordion] Does upgrading change my expiration date?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; No. Your expiration date stays the same as on the original plan.<br>&gt; [accordion] Are my Credits prorated by remaining days when I upgrade?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; No. The full monthly allowance of the new plan is issued immediately on upgrade, regardless of where in the cycle you upgrade.<br>&gt; [accordion] Can I revert to the old plan after upgrading?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; No. Upgrading is one-way and cannot be reverted to the Coding Plan.<br>&gt; [accordion] Can I change tiers at the same time as upgrading?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Not currently. The upgrade only switches you from the old plan to the same tier of the new plan. To move to a higher tier, complete the upgrade first and then change tiers, paying the prorated difference.<br>&gt; [accordion] How much is a Credit worth?<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Credits convert to payment at approximately \$1 ≈ 7M Credits. For each tier's monthly Credit allowance and price, see the [Step Plan overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md#plan-tiers).<br>For more on Credits, monthly allowances, booster packs, and tiers, see the [Step Plan overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md).<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

You would then need to purchase the Token Plan to continue using Step Plan.</li><li>&gt; Your Coding Plan is cancelled. A cancelled Coding Plan cannot be resubscribed, so you would need to purchase the new Token Plan to keep using Step Plan. We recommend checking in advance that your payment method is valid and has sufficient funds.</li><li>&gt; No. Your expiration date stays the same as on the original plan.</li><li>&gt; No. The full monthly allowance of the new plan is issued immediately on upgrade, regardless of where in the cycle you upgrade.</li><li>&gt; [accordion] Can I revert to the old plan after upgrading?</li><li>&gt; No. Upgrading is one-way and cannot be reverted to the Coding Plan.</li><li>&gt; Not currently. The upgrade only switches you from the old plan to the same tier of the new plan. To move to a higher tier, complete the upgrade first and then change tiers, paying the prorated difference.</li><li>&gt; Credits convert to payment at approximately \$1 ≈ 7M Credits. For each tier's monthly Credit allowance and price, see the [Step Plan overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md#plan-tiers).</li><li>For more on Credits, monthly allowances, booster packs, and tiers, see the [Step Plan overview](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/overview.md).</li></ul></details> |

| 字段 | 变化 | 原值 | 新值 |
|---|---|---|---|
| access scope | 新增 | 未记录 | public |
| api mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • client = OpenAI(base_url="https://api.stepfun.ai/v1", api_key=STEPFUN_KEY) |
| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • ![](https://mintcdn.com/stepfun2/LB7Z4XEbvwu-9ERC/images/typing_stepfun_short.gif?s=b591b1f40f7fa762b45af9e9a4704ff3) |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Stream responses for a smoother experience<br>Long generations can take time—for example, a 1,000-word article may take 10 seconds or more. Without feedback, users may think the app is broken and leave.<br>Use UX cues to show progress and keep users engaged until the result is ready.<br>Traditional APIs return only once, so developers rely on loading states or skeleton screens to communicate that work is in progress.<br>Stepfun’s [chat completion API](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/chat/chat-completion-create.md) supports streaming, so you can render the text as it is produced (like a typewriter effect) and let users read along while the model generates.<br>![](https://mintcdn.com/stepfun2/LB7Z4XEbvwu-9ERC/images/typing_stepfun_short.gif?s=b591b1f40f7fa762b45af9e9a4704ff3)<br>## How to enable streaming<br>Pass `stream=True` to the Chat Completion API to enable streaming. The API returns SSE data; parse it and render the chunks to your UI.<br>### Code example<br>```python theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>from openai import OpenAI<br># Initialize Stepfun client<br>STEPFUN_KEY = "YOUR_STEP_API_KEY"<br>client = OpenAI(base_url="https://api.stepfun.ai/v1", api_key=STEPFUN_KEY)<br># Call the completion API with streaming<br>stream = client.chat.completions.create(<br>model="step-5-preview",<br>messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "How is the All Seasons hotel?"}],<br>stream=True,<br>)<br># Print/render streamed chunks<br>for chunk in stream:<br>if chunk.choices[0].delta.content is not None:<br>print(chunk.choices[0].delta.content, end="")<br>```<br>## Notes<br>* Even with streaming, there is still some latency. Use a loading indicator alongside streamed output so users receive immediate feedback.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| api mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • `wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio`<br>• &gt; For Step Plan, use `wss://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/realtime/audio`<br>• "wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio?model=stepaudio-2.5-tts",<br>• const url = 'wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio?model=stepaudio-2.5-tts'; |
| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | 列表（46 项）<details><summary>展开完整内容</summary><ul><li># Streaming Text-to-Speech</li><li>This API allows you to generate audio using our Streaming Text-to-Speech (TTS) model.</li><li>`wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio`</li><li>&gt; For Step Plan, use `wss://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/realtime/audio`</li><li>The name of the model to use. Currently supports `stepaudio-3-tts` and `stepaudio-2.5-tts`.</li><li>&gt; Note: The `stepaudio-2.5-tts` model may produce significantly lower quality results in WebSocket streaming mode compared to non-streaming HTTP requests. If latency is not a concern, we recommend using the standard non-streaming speech synthesis API.</li><li>To generate audio in streaming mode, you must send the corresponding Client Event after the WebSocket connection is successfully established. The server will then return the corresponding Server Event, through which the audio is generated.</li><li>![Client Event and Server Event Mapping](https://mintcdn.com/stepfun2/LB7Z4XEbvwu-9ERC/images/tts/stream-tts2_en.png?fit=max\&amp;auto=format\&amp;n=LB7Z4XEbvwu-9ERC\&amp;q=85\&amp;s=2129a6f02fe1b4110860aa6539fbc505)</li><li>&#124; Send Text &#124; tts.text.delta &#124; tts.response.audio.delta &#124; Sends text increments. The corresponding audio delta is returned and can be played immediately. &#124;</li><li>&#124; Flush Buffer &#124; tts.text.flush &#124; tts.text.flushed &#124; Quickly clears the buffer and returns all remaining audio that has not yet been sent. &#124;</li><li>&#124; Text Done &#124; tts.text.done &#124; tts.response.audio.done &#124; Marks the end of this generation task. No further audio will be produced, and the server will release the connection. &#124;</li><li>&#124; Audio Error &#124; - &#124; tts.response.error &#124; Returned when audio generation encounters an error. &#124;</li><li>The event used to create a new session. After the WebSocket connection is established and the `tts.connection.done` Server Event is received, the client should send this event to initiate audio generation.</li><li>The audio format. Supported values are: `wav`, `mp3`, `flac`, `opus`, `pcm`. Also supports streaming variants: `mp3_stream`, `opus_stream`, `flac_stream`. Defaults to `mp3`.</li><li>The sampling rate of the output audio. Supported values: `8000`, `16000`, `22050`, `24000`, `48000`. Default: `24000`.</li><li>Global natural language guidance. Only effective when connected to the `stepaudio-2.5-tts` or `stepaudio-3-tts` model. Used to set the overall emotional tone for the session. Maximum length: 200 characters for `stepaudio-2.5-tts`, 500 characters for `stepaudio-3-tts`.</li><li>Default mode is designed for scenarios where TTS is used together with a large language model. In this mode, the system automatically buffers and segments sentences. Therefore, it does not return audio immediately; generation begins only when the accumulated input forms a complete sentence. If you need to force an immediate return, you can send `tts.text.flush`, and the model will promptly return the available audio.</li><li>Sentence mode is suitable for scenarios where the full text is already available. In this mode, the system automatically segments the text based on punctuation marks such as `. ! ?` and generates audio accordingly.</li><li>Example (stepaudio-2.5-tts):</li><li>### Generate Audio `tts.text.delta`</li><li>Client Event used to generate audio. During generation, if the TTS engine determines that the conditions for audio generation have been met, it returns a `tts.response.sentence.start` event to indicate that inference has begun. It then returns one or more `tts.response.audio.delta` events containing the audio data. After all audio for the sentence has been sent, the engine returns a `tts.response.sentence.end` event to indicate that the sentence has finished generating.</li><li>The text to be synthesized. The maximum length is 1,000 characters. When using `stepaudio-2.5-tts` or `stepaudio-3-tts`, supports using `()` to pass non-spoken inline instructions. If the text itself needs to be spoken, do not wrap it in parentheses.</li><li>Forces the TTS engine to return all audio generated so far by clearing the internal buffer.</li><li>### Finish Audio Generation `tts.text.done`</li><li>Complete Audio Generation.</li><li>### Receive Generated Audio `tts.response.audio.delta`</li><li>Indicates that the server is returning a chunk of generated audio.</li><li>Must be set to `tts.response.audio.delta`.</li><li>* `audio` `string` **required**</li><li>The Base64-encoded audio data.</li><li>The duration of this audio chunk, in seconds.</li><li>"type": "tts.response.audio.delta",</li><li>"audio": "BASE64 audio data",</li><li>### Generation Completed `tts.response.audio.done`</li><li>Indicates that the audio generation task has been completed. After receiving this event, the connection will automatically close. Additionally, if the connection remains idle for more than 60 seconds, the system will also complete the generation and close the connection.</li><li>Must be set to `tts.response.audio.done`.</li><li>The Base64-encoded audio data, containing all audio content generated in this session.</li><li>"type": "tts.response.audio.done",</li><li>"audio": ""</li><li>Indicates that an error occurred during audio generation.</li><li>"wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio?model=stepaudio-2.5-tts",</li><li>const url = 'wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio?model=stepaudio-2.5-tts';</li><li>### stepaudio-2.5-tts Code Example (Python)</li><li># 3. Send flush to force return current audio</li><li># Print server events (including generated audio data)</li><li># Use stepaudio-2.5-tts model in the connection URL</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Streaming Text-to-Speech<br>This API allows you to generate audio using our Streaming Text-to-Speech (TTS) model.<br>## Request Method<br>WebSocket<br>## Endpoint<br>`wss://api.stepfun.ai/v1/realtime/audio`<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For Step Plan, use `wss://api.stepfun.ai/step_plan/v1/realtime/audio`<br>## Request Headers<br>* `Authorization` `string` **required**<br>The API key used for authentication. Its value should be: `Bearer YOUR_STEP_API_KEY`.<br>## Request Body<br>* `model` `string` **required**<br>The name of the model to use. Currently supports `stepaudio-3-tts` and `stepaudio-2.5-tts`.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; The `step-tts-vivid` model name is deprecated but existing user requests will continue to be supported.<br>&gt; [!WARNING]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Note: The `stepaudio-2.5-tts` model may produce significantly lower quality results in WebSocket streaming mode compared to non-streaming HTTP requests. If latency is not a concern, we recommend using the standard non-streaming speech synthesis API.<br>## Call Instructions<br>To generate audio in streaming mode, you must send the corresponding Client Event after the WebSocket connection is successfully established. The server will then return the corresponding Server Event, through which the audio is generated.<br>If there is no activity for 60 consecutive seconds, the system will automatically close the connection.<br>### Client Event &amp; Server Event Mapping<br>A detailed description can be found in the explanation below.<br>![Client Event and Server Event Mapping](https://mintcdn.com/stepfun2/LB7Z4XEbvwu-9ERC/images/tts/stream-tts2_en.png?fit=max\&amp;auto=format\&amp;n=LB7Z4XEbvwu-9ERC\&amp;q=85\&amp;s=2129a6f02fe1b4110860aa6539fbc505)<br>&#124; Message Type &#124; Client Event &#124; Server Event &#124; Description &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; Connection Established &#124; - &#124; tts.connection.done &#124; Sent by the server after the WebSocket connection is successfully established. &#124;<br>&#124; Create Session &#124; tts.create &#124; tts.response.created &#124; Creates a new session. After receiving this response, the client may proceed with generation. &#124;<br>&#124; Sentence Start &#124; - &#124; tts.response.sentence.start &#124; Triggered when accumulated text meets the generation threshold and sentence generation begins. &#124;<br>&#124; Send Text &#124; tts.text.delta &#124; tts.response.audio.delta &#124; Sends text increments. The corresponding audio delta is returned and can be played immediately. &#124;<br>&#124; Sentence End &#124; - &#124; tts.response.sentence.end &#124; Triggered when accumulated text meets the end-of-sentence condition. &#124;<br>&#124; Flush Buffer &#124; tts.text.flush &#124; tts.text.flushed &#124; Quickly clears the buffer and returns all remaining audio that has not yet been sent. &#124;<br>&#124; Text Done &#124; tts.text.done &#124; tts.response.audio.done &#124; Marks the end of this generation task. No further audio will be produced, and the server will release the connection. &#124;<br>&#124; Audio Error &#124; - &#124; tts.response.error &#124; Returned when audio generation encounters an error. &#124;<br>## Client Event Details<br>### Create Session `tts.create`<br>The event used to create a new session. After the WebSocket connection is established and the `tts.connection.done` Server Event is received, the client should send this event to initiate audio generation.<br>* `type` `string` **required**<br>Must be set to `tts.create`.<br>* `data` `object` **required**<br>Event payload.<br>* `session_id` `string` **required**<br>The session ID used to identify which conversation the request belongs to. Returned by the `tts.connection.done` Server Event.<br>* `voice_id` `string` **required**<br>The ID of the voice to use. Refer to the System Voice ID List for supported voices and samples.<br>* `language` `string` **optional**<br>Target language; defaults to English when omitted. Supported values: `zh`, `en`, `ja`, `ko`, `fr`, `es`. Japanese, Korean, French, and Spanish are in preview.<br>* `response_format` `string` **optional**<br>The audio format. Supported values are: `wav`, `mp3`, `flac`, `opus`, `pcm`. Also supports streaming variants: `mp3_stream`, `opus_stream`, `flac_stream`. Defaults to `mp3`.<br>* `sample_rate` `int` **optional**<br>The sampling rate of the output audio. Supported values: `8000`, `16000`, `22050`, `24000`, `48000`. Default: `24000`.<br>* `pronunciation_map` `object array` **optional**<br>Defines a pronunciation rule to annotate or override the reading of specific characters or symbols. In Chinese text, tones are represented by numbers: 1 for the first tone, 2 for the second tone, 3 for the third tone, 4 for the fourth tone, and 5 for the neutral tone.<br>* `tone` `string` **required**<br>Specific pronunciation mapping rules, separated by `/`. Example: `["omg/oh my god"]`.<br>* `speed_ratio` `float` **optional**<br>The speaking rate. Valid range: 0.5 to 2.0. Default: 1.0.<br>* `volume_ratio` `float` **optional**<br>The volume level. Valid range: 0.1 to 2.0. Default: 1.0.<br>* `mode` `string` **optional**<br>The generation mode. Supported values: `default` (character-level generation, suitable for LLM real-time streaming scenarios) and `sentence` (sentence-level generation, suitable when full sentences are already prepared). Default: `default`.<br>* `instruction` `string` **optional**<br>Global natural language guidance. Only effective when connected to the `stepaudio-2.5-tts` or `stepaudio-3-tts` model. Used to set the overall emotional tone for the session. Maximum length: 200 characters for `stepaudio-2.5-tts`, 500 characters for `stepaudio-3-tts`.<br>* `markdown_filter` `bool` **optional**<br>Whether to enable Markdown filtering for the input text.<br>Default mode is designed for scenarios where TTS is used together with a large language model. In this mode, the system automatically buffers and segments sentences. Therefore, it does not return audio immediately; generation begins only when the accumulated input forms a complete sentence. If you need to force an immediate return, you can send `tts.text.flush`, and the model will promptly return the available audio.<br>Sentence mode is suitable for scenarios where the full text is already available. In this mode, the system automatically segments the text based on punctuation marks such as `. ! ?` and generates audio accordingly.<br>Example:<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"type": "tts.create",<br>"data": {<br>"session_id": "01956e7388477cfcbdc3aaabf364bc70",<br>"voice_id": "lively-girl",<br>"response_format": "wav",<br>"volume_ratio": 1.0,<br>"speed_ratio": 1.0,<br>"sample_rate": 16000,<br>"pronunciation_map": {<br>"tone": [<br>"LOL/laugh out loudly"<br>]<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>Example (stepaudio-2.5-tts):<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"type": "tts.create",<br>"data": {<br>"session_id": "01956e7388477cfcbdc3aaabf364bc70",<br>"voice_id": "zixinnansheng",<br>"response_format": "wav", |

Any other value is rejected with `400 voice_id_invalid`. To list your cloned voices, see [List cloned voices](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/api-reference/audio/list-voice.md).</li></ul></details> |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Get system voices<br>## Overview<br>Use this API to retrieve the system default voices available for a given model. The response includes a list of voice IDs and detailed information (name, description, recommended scenarios) for each voice.<br>When exposed to an agent, the agent can use this catalog to pick the most appropriate voice for a given scenario.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; This endpoint is the authoritative source for which voices your account can actually use. Voice IDs from other sources are rejected — see [Notes](#notes).<br>## Request<br>### Method<br>`GET`<br>### Endpoint<br>```text theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>GET https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/system_voices<br>```<br>### Query parameters<br>&#124; Parameter &#124; Type &#124; Required &#124; Allowed values &#124; Description &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; model &#124; string &#124; Yes &#124; `stepaudio-2.5-tts` &#124; The TTS model whose system voices to query. Any other value — including `stepaudio-2.5-realtime` — returns `400 invalid model`. &#124;<br>Omitting `model` returns `400` with `model is required`.<br>### Request example<br>```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>curl "https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/audio/system_voices?model=stepaudio-2.5-tts" \<br>-H "Authorization: Bearer $STEPFUN_API_KEY"<br>```<br>## Response<br>### Format<br>`JSON`<br>### Response fields<br>&#124; Field &#124; Type &#124; Description &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; voices &#124; array\[string] &#124; List of system voice IDs available under this model. &#124;<br>&#124; voices-details &#124; object &#124; Details per voice, keyed by voice ID. &#124;<br>&#124; voices-details.\&lt;id&gt;.voice-name &#124; string &#124; Voice display name. May be empty for some voices. &#124;<br>&#124; voices-details.\&lt;id&gt;.voice-description &#124; string &#124; Voice description, including gender and character. &#124;<br>&#124; voices-details.\&lt;id&gt;.recommended\_scene &#124; string &#124; Recommended use cases, separated by `、`. &#124;<br>### Response example<br>```json theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>{<br>"voices": [<br>"elegantgentle-female",<br>"lively-girl",<br>"livelybreezy-female",<br>"magnetic-voiced-male",<br>"soft-spoken-gentleman",<br>"vibrant-youth",<br>"zixinnansheng"<br>],<br>"voices-details": {<br>"elegantgentle-female": {<br>"voice-name": "气质温婉",<br>"voice-description": "女，真诚温柔，亲和力强，给人安全感",<br>"recommended_scene": "客服与业务办理、口播（解说、新闻）、教育与培训、情感陪伴"<br>},<br>"lively-girl": {<br>"voice-name": "Lively Girl",<br>"voice-description": "女，英文音色，亲和感，有活力",<br>"recommended_scene": "有声书、视频配音、语音助手"<br>},<br>"livelybreezy-female": {<br>"voice-name": "活力轻快",<br>"voice-description": "女，音色具有感染力、说服力和亲和力，有活力",<br>"recommended_scene": "情感陪伴、教育与培训、营销"<br>},<br>"magnetic-voiced-male": {<br>"voice-name": "Magnetic-voiced Male",<br>"voice-description": "男，英文音色，强烈的沉稳厚重感",<br>"recommended_scene": "有声书、视频配音"<br>},<br>"soft-spoken-gentleman": {<br>"voice-name": "Soft-spoken Gentleman",<br>"voice-description": "男，英文音色，沉稳温柔，给人安全感",<br>"recommended_scene": "情感陪伴、有声书"<br>},<br>"vibrant-youth": {<br>"voice-name": "Vibrant Youth",<br>"voice-description": "男，英文音色，温柔亲和",<br>"recommended_scene": "有声书、视频配音、语音助手"<br>},<br>"zixinnansheng": {<br>"voice-name": "自信男声",<br>"voice-description": "男，真诚亲和，有活力",<br>"recommended_scene": "有声书、情感陪伴、教育与培训、营销"<br>}<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; `voice-description` and `recommended_scene` are returned in Chinese as-is from the service, and `voice-name` is Chinese for voices that do not have an English name. Use the voice ID (the key under `voices-details`) when calling the TTS API. See the table below for an English reference.<br>### Voice reference<br>The same seven voices in English, in the order returned by the API. Four of them (`lively-girl`, `magnetic-voiced-male`, `soft-spoken-gentleman`, `vibrant-youth`) are purpose-built English voices.<br>&#124; Voice ID &#124; Voice &#124; Recommended use cases &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; `elegantgentle-female` &#124; Elegant, gentle female voice — sincere and reassuring &#124; Customer service, voice-over, education, emotional companionship &#124;<br>&#124; `lively-girl` &#124; Bright, lively female voice (English) &#124; Audiobook, video dubbing, voice assistant &#124;<br>&#124; `livelybreezy-female` &#124; Light, breezy female voice — engaging and persuasive &#124; Emotional companionship, education, marketing &#124;<br>&#124; `magnetic-voiced-male` &#124; Deep, magnetic male voice (English) &#124; Audiobook, video dubbing &#124; |

The request format is similar to the Chat Completion API.<br>## Endpoint<br>`POST https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/token/count`<br>## Request parameters<br>* `model` `string` ***required***\<br>Model name to use.<br>* `messages` `object array` ***required***\<br>Conversation so far, including user and assistant messages.<br>- System message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Always `system`.<br>* `content` `string`\<br>System message text.<br>- User message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Always `user`.<br>* `content` `string or object array`\<br>User message content: either a `multipart` list or a plain text string.<br>* `Plain text` `string`\<br>Directly pass a string as the message content.<br>* `multipart` list `object array`\<br>Structured multimodal message.<br>* Text message `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `text`.<br>* `text` `string`\<br>Text content.<br>* Image message `object`<br>* `type` `string`\<br>Always `image_url`.<br>* `image_url` `object`<br>* `url` `string`\<br>Image URL or base64 string.<br>URLs support only http and https.<br>Base64 example: `data:image/jpeg;base64,${base64_string}` (replace jpeg with your format and the encoded data).<br>Supported image formats: jpg/jpeg, png, webp, static gif.<br>- Assistant message `object`<br>* `role` `string`\<br>Always `assistant`.<br>* `content` `string &#124; null`\<br>Assistant message text.<br>## Response<br>* `data` `object`\<br>Token count result.<br>* `total_tokens` `int`\<br>Total tokens in the request.<br>## Examples<br>&gt; [tab] Text chat<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; curl https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/token/count \<br>&gt;   -H "Content-Type: application/json" \<br>&gt;   -H "Authorization: Bearer $STEP_API_KEY" \<br>&gt;   -d '{<br>&gt;     "model": "step-5-preview",<br>&gt;     "messages": [<br>&gt;       {<br>&gt;         "role": "system",<br>&gt;         "content": "You are an AI assistant provided by StepFun. You excel at conversations in Chinese, English, and other languages. While keeping user data safe, you should answer quickly and accurately, and reject any content related to pornography, drugs, gambling, violence, or terrorism."<br>&gt;       },<br>&gt;       {<br>&gt;         "role": "user",<br>&gt;         "content": "Hi, please introduce StepFun'\''s AI!"<br>&gt;       }<br>&gt;     ]<br>&gt;   }'<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```json filename="Response" theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; {<br>&gt;   "data": {<br>&gt;     "total_tokens": 85<br>&gt;   }<br>&gt; }<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] Mixed media<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={"theme":{"light":"light-plus","dark":"dark-plus"}}<br>&gt; curl https://api.stepfun.ai/v1/token/count \<br>&gt;   -H "Content-Type: application/json" \<br>&gt;   -H "Authorization: Bearer $STEP_API_KEY" \<br>&gt;   -d '{<br>&gt;     "model": "step-5-preview",<br>&gt;     "messages": [<br>&gt;       {<br>&gt;         "role": "system",<br>&gt;         "content": "You are an AI assistant provided by StepFun.
